patippurai
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimaiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷaiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolainōkkup pārvaiyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. 
 tantai periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷkai
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkaikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭu attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷaip patittu varukiṟōm.
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉaivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum cērttu avaruṭaiya maṟaivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivanta, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭuraikaḷait tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nilai aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉaivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭuraip putaiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇaiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷai naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉaivukūrkiṟōm.
collārāyccit tuṟaiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamaiyuṭaivar eṉṟu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭaikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟaiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñarai iṉṉum peṟṟut taravillai eṉṟu paṉmoḻi
aṟiñar appātturaiyarum, kuṟaimatiyar tēkkivaitta karaiyiruḷai nīkka vanta maṟaimalaiyār vaḻivanta niṟaimalaiyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravaittalaivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉaivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa ceytikaḷai ikkaḷañciyattai paṭippār aṉaivarum karuttil koḷḷa vēṇṭukiṟōm.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉaiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam uraippaṉa. tamiḻai āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kākkum ariya oḷiñāyiṟākum. vīḻntupaṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimaippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻ
moḻiyai mīṭkavallatu ivartam nūlkaḷākum eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟai ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamaiyākum.
pāvāṇarait tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum eṉṟa maṉauṇarvōṭu, pāvāṇar koḷkaikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilaittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷaiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talaippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷai veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟaikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟaiyum, citaikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyaiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putaiyalkaḷai tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyai mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻupōtum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻi ñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷai vāṅki payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamaiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷai ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉaivil kūṟukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.

vāṉmaḻai vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyiraip paṇayam vaittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷai eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷai iṭaiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vaippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇai
yākavum ivai iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vaippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avai cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟaik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟamainta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷai īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻaipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟait tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakaiya pēṇutal uṭaiyavai eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavai tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kaippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭamai kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varaivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamaiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamaikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakaiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vaitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭaikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamaiyallavō!
akkaṭamaiyai avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇaiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvai! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭai puritalukku mēṟkoṭai illaiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavai pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭurai poḻivu mutalām vakaiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varaivu pā varaivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟai ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻai vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭamai puriyum vīṟuṭaiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakaiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭaiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭaippukaḷai mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vaikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippai vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.



mukavurai
illaṟamākiya nallaṟam pūṇum makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyil tirumaṇamē talaiciṟanta maṅkala nikaḻcciyātalālum, nīṇṭa kālamākat tamiḻukkum tamiḻaṉukkum iḻukkunērum vaṇṇam āriyamuṟaiyil perumpāl tamiḻa maṇaṅkaḷ naṭaipeṟṟu varuvatālum, aṇmaiyil yāṉ naṭattivaitta pala tirumaṇaṅkaḷil yāṉ uṇarnta kuṟaiyai niṟaittaṟ poruṭṭum, in nūl eḻutappeṟṟatu.
cēlam, 15-5-56	tēvanēyaṉ

tamiḻar tirumaṇam



muṉṉurai
1. vāḻkkai nōkkam
iv vulakiṟ piṟanta x›bvāruvarumoru ciṟintaṉumiṉgmāyvāHviru«puvatālum,av iṉgttiṟkuiṉ¿yamaiyātuvē©ṭuvatupehruḷātalālum,ap pehruḷpe‰wrceyavēṇṭiyakaṭakaṟamahtalālumimkyilmakkaŸvāḻ¡kaikkuṟiknkāḷiṉpaKmpoUSmaṟamum eṉamū‹ṟāfkkuṟittadrmuṉṉēhr.immū‹wDŸS«, aṟam ciṟantatāyum ēṉai yiraṇṭiṟkum potuvāyu miruttalāṉ, aṟavaḻiyil īṭṭiya poruḷaikkoṇṭu aṟavaḻiyil iṉpan tuykkavēṇṭu meṉṉum karuttāl, aṟattai muṉvaittu mupporuḷaiyum aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉa māṟṟiyamaittaṉar piṉṉōr.
oruvarkku ūṇ, icai, kāṭci mutaliya piṟavaṟṟālum iṉpamuṇṭāku mēṉum, pēraḷavupaṟṟiyum aimpulaṉun taḻuval paṟṟiyum, peṇṇiṉpamē immaiyiṟ pēriṉpamākak koḷḷappeṟṟatu.
peṇṇiṟ peruntakka yāvuḷa kaṟpeṉṉun
tiṇmaiyuṇ ṭākap peṟiṉ.	(54)
kaṇṭukēṭ ṭuṇṭuyirt tuṟṟaṟiyum aimpulaṉum
oṇṭoṭi kaṇṇē yuḷa.	(111)
eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvaṉāruṅ kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
makkaḷiṭai matattuṟai vaḷarcciyaṭaintu vīṭupēṟṟu nampikkai ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, māntaṉ kuṟikkōḷ allatu pēṟu aṟamporuḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉa nāṉkāka vakukkap peṟṟatu. in nāṉkum nāṟporuḷ allatu nāṉmāṇ poruḷ eṉappaṭum.
immaiyiṉpamākiya peṇṇiṉpamum maṟumaiyiṉpamākiya vīṭṭiṉpamum, oppu nōkka vakaiyāl, muṟaiyē ciṟṟiṉpam pēriṉpam eṉappaṭiṉum, ciṟṟiṉpamē iyaṟkaik kēṟṟatum, uṭaṉē nukartaṟ kuriyatum, eḷitāyk kiṭṭuvatum, kaṇkūṭākak kāṇap peṟuvatu māyiruttaliṉ, atuvē perumpālarāl virumpappaṭuvatām.
ītalaṟam tīviṉaiviṭ ṭīṭṭalporuḷ eññāṉṟum
kātal maṉaiyāḷum kātalaṉum - tītiṉṟip
paṭṭatē iṉpam paraṉainiṉain timmūṉṟum 
viṭṭatē pēriṉpa vīṭu
eṉṉum auvaiyār ceyyuḷ, nāṟporuḷiyalpaiyum naṉkaṉam terivikkum. ic ceyyuḷiṉ mutalaṭiyai, ītalaṟam tīviṉaiviṭ ṭīṭṭal poru''ḷeṉṟum eṉṟu cilar tiruttuvar.
cila tuṟaviyar, varaiyiṟantu peṇṇai veṟuttup peṇṇiṉpattaip paḻittiruppar. atu tuṟaviyarkkē kūṟiyateṉṟu illaṟattār poruṭ paṭuttātu viṭṭuviṭal vēṇṭum.  vilaimakaḷuṟavaiyum neṟitiṟampiya kāma nukarcciyaiyum paḻikkalāmēyaṉṟi, peṇṇiṉpantaṉṉaiyē paḻittal kūṭātu. aṅṅaṉam paḻippavar, iṟaivaṉ ēṟpāṭṭaiyum tam peṟṟōr vāḻkkaiyaiyum paḻippavarēyāvar.
2. vāḻkkai vakai
māntaṉ vāḻkkai, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam eṉa iru vakaittu, maṉaivi yōṭu kūṭi illattiliruntu, ataṟkuriya aṟañceytu vāḻum vāḻkkai illaṟam; ulakappaṟṟait tuṟantu, ataṟkuriya aṟattōṭu kūṭik kāṭṭil tavañ ceytu vāḻum vāḻkkai tuṟavaṟam. atu maṇavānilaiyilum toṭaṅkalām; maṇanta nilaiyilum toṭaṅkalām.
oruvaṉ maṇañ ceyyātu iṟutivarai māṇiyāy (piramaccāriyāy) iruppiṉum, avaṉ vāḻkkai illaṟattiṉ pāṟpaṭum.
oruvaṉ nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu nīṅkik kāṭṭil vāḻiṉum, maṉaiyāḷoṭu kūṭi vāḻiṉ, atu illaṟattiṉ pāṟpaṭuvatē.
oruvaṉ illattiliruntu maṉaiyāḷoṭu kūṭi vāḻiṉum, aṟañ ceyyātu iruppiṉ, avaṉ vāḻkkai illaṟamākātu veṟumaṉāṉa ilvāḻkkaiyām. oruvaṉ tuṟavu mēṟkoṇṭum ataṟkuriya aṟam pūṇāṉāyiṉ, atu īraṟattoṭuṅ kūṭātu tīviṉaiyai mikukkum alvāḻkkaiyām.
ikkālattil, tuṟavu pūṇṭōr kāṭṭiṟkuc cellātu nāṭṭilum nakarattilum taṅki, tammāliyaṉṟavarai potumakkaṭkut toṇṭu ceyvatu, ciṟanta tuṟavākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
3. illaṟac ciṟappu
iṟaivaṉ ēṟpāṭṭiṉpaṭi makkaḷulakam iṭaiyaṟātu toṭarntu varuvataṟku illaṟamē kāraṇamātalālum, tuṟaviyarkkum avar muṟṟat tuṟakkumvarai iṉṟiyamaiyāt tuṇaiyāyiruppatu illaṟattārē yātalālum, illaṟattālum vīṭupēṟu kiṭṭumātalālum, māyamāla naṭippiṟkiṭam tuṟavaṟattiṉum illaṟattiṟ kuṟaitalālum, illaṟamē nallaṟamām. itaṉālaṉṟō,
illaṟa mallatu nallaṟa maṉṟu
eṉa auvaiyārum,
aṟaṉeṉap paṭṭatē yilvāḻkkai yaḵtum
piṟaṉpaḻippa tillāyiṉ naṉṟu,	(49)
aṟattāṟṟiṉ ilvāḻkkai yāṟṟiṟ puṟattāṟṟiṟ
pōoyp peṟuva tevaṉ	(46)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārum, kūṟiṉār eṉka.
aṉpuṭaimai, viruntōmpal, iṉiyavai kūṟal, ceynnaṉṟi yaṟital, naṭuvunilaimai, aṭakkamuṭaimai, oḻukkamuṭaimai, piṟaṉil viḻaiyāmai, poṟaiyuṭaimai, aḻukkāṟāmai, veḵkāmai, puṟaṅkūṟāmai, payaṉila collāmai, tīviṉaiyaccam, oppuravaṟital, īkai eṉpaṉa illaṟa vāḻkkaikku vitikkappaṭṭa aṟaṅkaḷātaliṉ, ivaiyaṉaittaiyuṅ kaikkoḷvār vīṭṭulakai yaṭaitaṟku eḷḷaḷavum aiyamiṉṟām.
āyiṉum, makkaḷtokai mikku mānila muḻutum iṭarppaṭum ikkālattil, tuṟavaṟañ ciṟantateṉṟu kūṟit tuṟaviyarai ūkkutal vēṇṭum eṉṟē tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
4. tirumaṇamuṅ karaṇamum
ōr āṭavaṉum oru peṇṭum, kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyumāka illaṟam naṭatta icaintu oṉṟucērvatē maṇamām. maṇattal kalattal allatu kūṭutal. maṇavāḻkkaikkeṉṟē iṟaivaṉ makkaḷai āṇum peṇṇumāyp paṭaittiruppatālum, ataṉiṭattu mikunta aṟappoṟuppuḷḷamaiyālum, atu āyiraṅkālattup payir ākaiyālum, vāḻkkait tuṇaivar iruvarum teyvattiṉmuṉ allatu teyvattiṉpēril palaraṟiya āṇaiyiṭṭuk kūṭuvatālum, maṇam teyvattaṉmai peṟṟut tirumaṇam eṉap peṟṟatu. ip peyar, piṉṉar, tirumaṇat toṭarpāṉa viḻāvaiyuṅ kuṟittatu.
tirumaṇattiṟkuriya oppanta allatu tālikaṭṭuc caṭaṅku karaṇam eṉappaṭum. karaṇam ceykai. atu āṭcipaṟṟic caṭaṅkai uṇarttiṟṟu. karaṇattōṭu kūṭiya tirumaṇattai vatuvai maṇam eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakku.
mutaṟ kālattil, ellā āṭavarum peṇṭirum, paruvam vantapiṉ vilaṅkum paṟavaiyumpōlak karaṇamiṉṟiyē kūṭi vāḻntu vantaṉar. āyiṉ, cila āṭavar, tām maṇanta makaḷirai maṇakkavillaiyeṉṟu poyyuraittum, avaraik kaiviṭṭum, avar vāḻvaik keṭuttum vantatiṉāl, makkaḷmītu aruḷkoṇṭa tamiḻa muṉivar, karaṇam eṉṉum tirumaṇac caṭaṅkai ēṟpaṭuttiṉar. maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷait taṉ nilaiyāṉa vāḻkkait tuṇaiyākak koḷvatāka, palaraṟiyak kaṭavuḷ tirumuṉ cūḷ (āṇai) iṭuvatē karaṇamām.
poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa	(kaṟpiyal, 4)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam
5. maṇamakkaḷ peyar
kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyumāka oruvarukkoruvar vāḻkkait tuṇaivarāy illaṟan toṭaṅkum iruvarum, tirumaṇattaṉṟu maṇamakkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. av viruvaraiyum pirittuk kūṟuṅkāl, maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷ eṉṟum, maṇavāḷaṉ maṇavāṭṭi eṉṟum, peṇ māppiḷḷai (maṇavāḷap piḷḷai) eṉṟum, peṇ piḷḷai eṉṟum, kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. maṇamakaṉaip piḷḷai eṉpatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku.
kaṇavaṉ maṉaivi, āṇmakaṉ peṇṭāṭṭi, akamuṭaiyāṉ aka muṭaiyāḷ, illāḷaṉ illāḷ eṉṉum peyariṇaikaḷ tirumaṇattiṟkup piṉpu illaṟakkālattil nilaiyāka vaḻaṅkuvaṉa. maṇāṭṭuppeṇ eṉpatu muṟaiyē māṭṭuppeṇ nāṭṭuppeṇ eṉa maruvi, marumakaḷ eṉṉum poruḷil, tañcai vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. talaivaṉ talaivi, talaimakaṉ talaimakaḷ, kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti (nāṭukiḻavōr) eṉpaṉa, aracak kulattārkkuriyaṉavāy, illaṟam neṭukalum vaḻaṅkum ilakkiya vaḻakkām. ivaṟṟuḷ, kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti eṉpaṉa maṉaikkiḻavaṉ maṉaikkiḻatti eṉṉum vaḻakkil potumakkaṭkum vaḻaṅkum. maṉaikkiḻattiyai vāḻvaraci eṉpatu nellaināṭṭu nalvaḻakku.
tiruvaḷḷuvar, kaṇavaṉukkut talaimai tōṉṟa maṉaiviyai vāḻkkait tuṇaiyeṉṟu kūṟiyiruppiṉum, illaṟattai irupakaṭ ṭorucakaṭu eṉṟum, kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyar kātaloṟṟumaiyai ōrāviṉuk kirukōṭu tōṉṟiṉāṟpōl eṉṟum, uvamittuk kūṟum vaḻakkiṟkum. ikkālat tōṅkivarum camanilaik karuttiṟkum ēṟpa, iruvaraiyum taṉittaṉi vāḻkkait tuṇaiyeṉṟō, vāḻkkait tuṇaivaṉ vāḻkkait tuṇaivi eṉṟō, kuṟippatu kuṟṟamākātu.
6. aṉpum kātalum kāmamum
aṉpu kātal kāmam eṉpaṉa, parunōkkil oṉṟāyiṉum, nuṇ ṇōkkil vēṟupaṭṭaṉa.
aṉpeṉpatu, ēcuvum puttarum pōl ellāriṭattum kāṭṭum nēyam. atu aṟamākavum aṟaviṉaikaṭkellām kāraṇamākavum karutappaṭum.
aṉpillā rellān tamakkuriyar aṉpuṭaiyār 
eṉpum uriyar piṟarkku	(72)
aṉpum aṟaṉum uṭaittāyiṉ ilvāḻkkai
paṇpum payaṉum atu	(45)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷkaḷ itai yuṇarttum.
kātal eṉpatu, oruvarai yoruvar iṉṟiyamaiyāk kaḻiperu nēyamāy iruvariṭai nikaḻvatu. atu, kōpperuñ cōḻaṉum picirāntaiyārum pōlum naṇpariṭattum, peṟṟōrum piḷḷaiyum pōlum uṟaviṉariṭattum, pūtappāṇṭiyaṉum avaṉ tēviyum pōlum kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyariṭattum, amaivatu. atu ariya piṟavik kuṇam.
kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum iṟantamai kēṭṭa tāymār uṭaluyir tuṟantamai kāṇka. kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyariṭaippaṭṭa kātal uṇmaiyil iruvarkkum potuvēṉum, atu ciṟappāka maṉaivikkē allatu maṉaivi yarkkē uriyatu eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa koḷkai, toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
kāmam eṉpatu, kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyariṭattēyē allatu āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupāliṭaiyēyē, nikaḻakkūṭiya ciṟappuvakai nēyam. kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyar illaṟa viṉpam tuyttaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa nēyam eṉṉum poruḷilēyē kāmam eṉṉuñ collai āṇṭu, iṉpattuppālaik kāmattuppāl eṉak kuṟittaṉar vaḷḷuvar. ac col, iṉṟu kalavi vēṭkai eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa poruḷil vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, aruḷ eṉpatu yātōveṉiṉ, atu uyarntōr tāḻntōriṭattuk kāṭṭum irakkam eṉka.

1. paṇṭait tamiḻmaṇam
paṇṭait tamiḻ maṇam eṉṟu iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭavai, tamiḻakattil, toṉṟutoṭṭup pirāmaṇar peruntokaiyāy vantu palavūrkaḷilum taṅkumaḷavum, tamiḻap pārppārālum kulattalaivarālum tamiḻil naṭattappeṟṟu vanta maṇaṅkaḷākum.
pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭu pukattoṭaṅkiyatu ēṟattāḻak ki.mu. 2000 eṉiṉum, avar mutalaṭiyilēyē peruntokaiyiṉarāy iṅku vantavarallar. mutaṉmutal iṅkuk kāl vaitta pirāmaṇar viralviṭṭu eṇṇattakkavarē yāvar. kiṟittava vūḻi toṭaṅkumvarai iṭaiyiṭṭiṭaiyiṭṭuc ciṟuciṟu kūṭṭattārākavē avar vantu koṇṭiruntaṉar. ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ, pallava aracar kālattiltāṉ, avar peruvāriyāka vaṭanāṭṭiliruntu kuṭiyēṟṟappaṭṭaṉar. itaip piṟkālac cēracōḻa pāṇṭiyarum piṉpaṟṟiṉar.
māmutu pārppāṉ maṟaivaḻi kāṭṭiṭa eṉṟu cilappatikāram pirāmaṇa allatu āriyap pārppaṉap purōkitattaik kuṟittiruppatu, kaṭaiccaṅkak kālattil nakaraṅkaḷilum mānakaraṅkaḷilum perumpālum aracariṭaiyum vaṇikariṭaiyum irunta nilaimaiyaittāṉ kuṟikkum. iṉṟum pirāmaṇarillāta pala nāṭṭuppuṟattūrkaḷ iruppatālum, cila tamiḻakkulattār pirāmaṇap purōkitamiṉṟit tam maṇaṅkaḷai naṭattikkoḷvatālum, pirāmaṇar vanta piṉpum, kaṭaiccaṅkak kālamvarai perumpāl tamiḻar maṇaṅkaḷ tamiḻ marapilēyē naṭantuvantaṉa eṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum.
tamiḻappārppār, paṇṭāram, pulavaṉ, kurukkaḷ, tiru(kkaḷ), pūcāri, uvaccaṉ, ōtuvāṉ, pōṟṟi, nampi, arumaikkāraṉ (puṭavaikkāraṉ), vaḷḷuvaṉ mutaliya palvēṟu vakuppār āvar. pārppāṉ kōyiṟ karumaṅkaḷaip pārppavaṉ.
antaṇaṉ, aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ mutaṉmutal tamiḻat tuṟavi yaraiyē kuṟittatupōṉṟu, pārppāṉ eṉṉum peyarum mutaṉmutal tamiḻap pūcāriyaiyē kuṟittatu. turai eṉṉum tamiḻ allatu teluṅkac col nam nāṭṭil taṅkum mēṉāṭṭārkku varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭatupōṉṟē, antaṇar, pārppār eṉṉum peyarkaḷum pirāmaṇarkku varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭaṉa eṉka. iṉṉum itaṉ virivaiyellām eṉatu tamiḻarkula marapu eṉṉum nūliṟ kaṇṭu koḷka. tamiḻaruḷ, pārppār eṉpār illaṟattārum, antaṇar eṉpār tuṟavaṟattārum āvar.
kulattalaivarāvār nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraṉ, nāṭṭāṉ, periya taṉakkāraṉ, ampalakkāraṉ, ūrkkavuṇṭaṉ, ūrkkuṭumpaṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ, ūrāḷi, mūppaṉ eṉa āṅkāṅku vevvēṟu peyarāl aḻaikkappeṟum kulaviyal ūrāṭciyāḷar.
paṇṭait tamiḻmaṇa muṟaiyilēyē iṉṉum pala maṇaṅkaḷ naṭantu varuvatāl, avaṟṟaiyun taḻuvumāṟu paṇṭait tamiḻ maṇam  eṉṉum talaippiṟkup paṇṭaik kālattil ellāt tamiḻarum kaiyāṇṭa maṇamuṟai eṉṟu poruḷ koḷka.
1. maṇavakai
(1) ulakiyaṟ pākupāṭu
paṇṭait tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷiṉ ulakiyaṟ pākupāṭu, piṉ varumāṟu palvēṟu muṟai paṟṟiyatākum.
1. koḷmuṟai paṟṟiyatu
paṇṭait tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ, peṇkōṭal muṟaipaṟṟi, (1)koṭaimaṇam, (2) kātal maṇam, (3) kavarvu (vaṉkōḷ)  maṇam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.
(1) koṭaimaṇam
koṭaimaṇamāvatu, maṇamakaṉēṉum avaṉ peṟṟōrēṉum maṇamakaḷ peṟṟōrai aṭuttuk kēṭka, avar koṭuppatu. atu, (1) tāṉak koṭai, (2) vilaikkoṭai, (3) nilaippāṭṭukkoṭai eṉa mūvakaittu.
tāṉakkoṭaiyāvatu, oruvar tam makaḷait takka ēḻai maṇāḷaṉukkut tāmē ellāc celavum ēṟṟu maṇañceytu vaippatu, vilaikkoṭaiyāvatu, oruvaṉ taṉ makaḷukku īṭāka oru tokaiyai oru celvaṉiṭam peṟṟukkoṇṭu, avaḷai avaṉukku maṉaiviyākak koṭuppatu; vilai viṟpaṉai; itu kīḻōriṭam aruki nikaḻvatu; nilaippāṭṭuk koṭaiyāvatu, oruvar tam makaḷai oruvaṉukku ētēṉum nilaippāṭu allatu akkuttup (condition) paṟṟi maṉaiviyākak koṭuppatu. an nilaippāṭu, ittuṇaip parican taralvēṇṭumeṉṟum, ittuṇaik kālam peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōrkku uḻaittalvēṇṭu meṉṟum, iṉṉa maṟacceyalaip purital vēṇṭu
meṉṟum, maṇamakaḷai iṉṉa kalaiyil vellutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṇattiṉpiṉ peṇ vīṭṭilēyē vatital vēṇṭumeṉṟum, palvēṟu tiṟappaṭṭatā yirukkum.
maṟacceyalkaḷ, kollēṟu kōṭal (ēṟu taḻuval), tiripaṉṟi yeytal, pulippāl kaṟattal, koṭuvilaṅku kōṟal, pakaivaraiyaḻittal, vilnāṇēṟ ṟal, peruṅkal tūkkal mutaliyaṉa. ēṟu - kāḷai, kōṭal - aṭakkal, 
kōṟal - kollutal. tiripaṉṟi yeytalāvatu, mikavuyarattil viraivākac cuḻaṉṟu koṇṭirukkum ciṟiya paṉṟiyuruvai orē taṭavaiyil eytu vīḻttutal.
paṇṭaik kālattil mullainilattilirunta āyariṭai, ēṟu taḻuvi maṇappatē kulamarapāka iruntuvantatu. ōr āyarpāṭiyil  allatu cēriyil, oru peṇkuḻantai piṟantavuṭaṉ ataṉ peyarukku oru cēṅkaṉṟaip peṟṟōr otukki vaippar. ak kaṉṟaik kāyaṭiyāmalum, vēlaiyil vayakkāmalum, koḻutta ūṭṭaṅ koṭuttuk kompu cīvik kūrākkuvar. āṇṭutōṟum, kuṟitta naṉṉāḷil, maṇapparuvamaṭainta maṅkaiyarkkuriya kāḷaikaḷaiyellām oru toḻuvattil aṭaittu vaittu, ovvoṉṟākat tiṟantuviṭuvar. makkaḷ āravārattaiyum ēṟukōṭpaṟai muḻakkattaiyum, kaṇṭum kēṭṭum, maruṇṭōṭum ovvoru kollēṟṟaiyum, māṇiyarāṉa āya iḷaiñar piṭittu niṟutta muyalvar. palar kollēṟukaḷāṟ kuttik kollappaṭuvatu muṇṭu. oru kollēṟṟai evaṉ piṭittaṭakki niṟuttu kiṉṟāṉō avaṉ av ēṟṟiṟkuriya āyamakaḷai maṇappāṉ. im maṇamuṟai, kalittokai eṉṉum caṅka nūlil, mullaikkaliyil, virivākak kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. vaṭanāṭṭiṟ kaṇṇaṉ ēḻ ēṟutaḻuvi nappiṉṉaiyai maṇantatu it tamiḻ marapē.
iṅṅaṉam oru maṟacceyalai maṇamakaṉatu takutiyākak koḷḷum vaḻakkattiṉāl, oru kulattāriṉ maṟattiṟam mēṉmēlum vaḷarntu varuvatuṭaṉ, avarkkup piṟakkum kuḻantaikaḷum iyalpāka maṟavuṇarcci viñciyavāyiruk
kiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu, maṟañciṟanta āṭavaraiyē peṇṭir maṇakkum ēṟpāṭṭai, pāliyal terippu (sexual selection) eṉpar ṭārviṉ pēraṟiñar. oru kulattār tamakkuḷḷēyē neṭukalum maṇantu varuvatāl ēṟpaṭum eccaviyaṟ kuṟai
pāṭṭiṟku īṭu ceyvatu, ēṟutaḻuval pōṉṟa maṇamakaṉ maṟaviyal takuti ēṟpāṭē.
ēṟu taḻuval eṉṉum paṇṭai vaḻakkamē, iṉṟu kaḷḷar maṟavariṭaic callikkaṭṭu eṉṟum, mañcuviraṭṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. āyar iv vaḻakkattai nīṇṭa kālamāka aṭiyōṭu viṭṭuviṭṭatiṉāl, tam muṉṉōriṉ maṟattai muṟṟum iḻantuviṭṭaṉar. callikkaṭṭu māṭṭut toḻuvai iṉṟum pāṭi allatu pāṭivācal eṉṟaḻaippatu, iv vaḻakkam paṇṭaik kālattil āyarpāṭiyil nikaḻntatu eṉpatai uṇarttum.
tirumaṇattaṉṟu maṇamakkaḷ ūrvalam varuvatum, cila ciṟṟūrkaḷil ayalūr maṇamakaṉ vantu oru peṇṇai maṇantu mīḷumpōtu, avvūr iḷaiñar iḷavaṭṭak kācu eṉṉum kainnīṭṭam kēṭpatum, paṇṭaik kālattil oru peṇṇai nōkkip pala iḷaiñar pōṭṭiyiṭṭup porutataik kuṟippāy uṇarttum.
āṇmakap pēṟillāta peṟṟōr, tam marumakaṉait tam illat tilēyē iruttikkoḷvar. iv ēṟpāṭṭai illattam eṉpar kaṉṉaṭar.
(2) kātal maṇam
kātal maṇamāvatu, ōr āṭavaṉum oru peṇṭum peṟṟōraik kēḷātum piṟarukkut teriyātum oruvaraiyoruvar kātalittu, tāmē kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyumākak kūṭikkoḷvatu. itu, kaḷavil toṭaṅkuvatum kaṟpil toṭaṅkuvatum eṉa iruvakaittu. kaḷaveṉpatu maṟaivu; kaṟpeṉpatu veḷippaṭai nī iṉṉavā ṟoḻuka vēṇṭum eṉa maṇamakaḷ tirumaṇa āciriyaṉāl kaṟpikkappaṭum nilaimai kaṟpu eṉpar.
kaḷavil toṭaṅkuvatu, irumāta ellaikkuḷ eṉṟēṉum veḷip paṭṭuk kaṟpāka māṟiviṭum. ik kaḷavu veḷippāṭu, (1) uṭaṉpōkku, (2) aṟattoṭu nilai eṉṉum iruvakaikaḷuḷ oṉṟāl ēṟpaṭum, uṭaṉpōk kāvatu, kaḷavoḻukkam taṭaippaṭṭaviṭattu allatu kātaliyiṉ peṟṟōr avaḷait tara icaiyāviṭattu, kātalaṉ avaḷai vēṟṟūrkkēṉum taṉ vīṭṭiṟkēṉum aḻaittukkoṇṭu pōyviṭal. aṟattoṭu nilaiyāvatu, kātaliyiṉ melivu kaṇṭu ataip pōkkuvataṟku, avaḷ peṟṟōr kaṭṭuvicci (kuṟikāri), vēlaṉ (mantirakkāraṉ) mutaliyōriṉ tuṇai vēṇṭum pōtō, ataṟku muṉṉatākavō, kātali tāṉākavēṉum taṉ tōḻi vāyilākavēṉum taṉ kātalaṉaippaṟṟit terivittal.
kātalar oru nāḷuṅ kaḷavoḻukkamiṉṟik kaṟpākavē tam kūṭṭu vāḻkkaiyait toṭaṅkuvatumuṇṭu. iv iruvakait toṭakkamum, karaṇattōṭu kūṭiyatākavum irukkalām; kūṭātatākavu mirukkalām. ak karaṇamum, maṇamakaṉ tāṉē ceyvippatākavu mirukkalām; maṇamakaḷ peṟṟōraik koṇṭu ceyvippatākavu mirukkalām.
iṅkuk kaṟpeṉpatu uṇmaiyil kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ veḷippāṭē yāyiṉum, atu potuvākak karaṇattoṭu kūṭiyatākavē koḷḷappaṭum.
kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu karaṇamoṭu puṇarak
koḷaṟkuri marapiṉ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyaik
koṭaikkuri marapiṉōr koṭuppakkoḷ vatuvē   (kaṟpiyal,1)
koṭuppōr iṉṟiyum karaṇam uṇṭē
puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya kālai yāṉa	(mēṟpaṭi 2)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam.
uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟa kātalaṉ, taṉṉūr vēṟṟūrāyiṉ pōṉaviṭattum kātali yūrēyāyiṉum, perumpālum taṉ maṉaiyilēyē atai vaittuk koḷvaṉ. uṟṟārk kuriyar poṟṟoṭi makaḷir eṉpatālum.
uyiriṉum ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē nāṇiṉum 
ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpucciṟan taṉṟu	(tol. kaḷavu. 22)
eṉpatālum, kātaliyiṉ peṟṟōr kātalaṉai oppukkoṇṭu, avaṉ vatuvai maṇattait tam maṉaiyil naṭatta virumpiṉ, av viruppam niṟaivēṟuvatu
muṇṭu. yār maṉaiyil vatuvai nikaḻiṉum, vatuvaikku muṉ maṇamakaḷ kālil avaḷ peṟṟōrāl aṇiyappaṭṭirunta cilampai nīkkutaṟku oru caṭaṅku ceyyappaṭum. atu cilampukaḻi nōṉpu eṉappaṭum.
itai,
nummaṉaic cilampu kaḻīi ayariṉum
emmaṉai vatuvai naṉmaṇaṅ kaḻikeṉac
colliṉ evaṉō maṟṟē veṉvēl
maiyaṟa viḷaṅkiya kaḻalaṭip
poyval kāḷaiyai yīṉṟa tāykkē	(399) 
eṉṉum aiṅkuṟunūṟṟuc ceyyuḷāl aṟiyalām.
itu, uṭaṉkoṇṭupōṉa kātalaṉ mīṇṭu vantu, taṉ kātaliyait taṉ illattiṟkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟa viṭattu, avaṉ tāy avaḷukkuc cilampukaḻi nōṉpu ceykiṉṟāḷeṉak kēṭṭa naṟṟāy (peṟṟa tāy), aṅku niṉṟum vantārkkuc colliyatu.
(num - um. cilampu - taṇṭai. kaḻīi - kaḻittu. ayariṉum - koṇṭāṭi ṉālum. kaḻikeṉa - naṭakkaveṉṟu. evaṉō - eṉṉa. veṉ - veṟṟi. maiyaṟa - kuṟṟam nīṅka, kaḻal - vīrakkālaṇi, kāḷai - vīraṉākiya kātalaṉ).
karaṇamiṉṟiyum kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyumāka iru kātalar icaintu vāḻak kūṭumāyiṉum, karaṇattoṭu toṭaṅkum illaṟamē ellārālum pōṟṟap paṭuvatām; aḵtillākkāl, atu vaippu eṉa iḻintōrālum tūṟṟap paṭuvatē.
kātalar vāḻvu kaḷavoḻukkattōṭu toṭaṅkiṉ meyyuṟu puṇarcciyum, allākkāl uḷḷappuṇarccimāttiraiyum, kaṟpiṟkumuṉ peṟupavarāvar.
peṟṟōrum piṟarum muṭittu vaikkum tirumaṇattilum maṇamakkaḷ iruvarkkum kātaluṇṭākalā meṉiṉum, kātal maṇameṉṟu ciṟappittuc collappeṟuvatu ōr āṭavaṉum oru peṇṭum tāmāka vāḻkkai oppantañ ceytukoḷvatē.
maṭalēṟṟam
mutupaḻaṅkālattil, oru kaṭuṅkātalaṉ allatu kātaṟpittaṉ avaṉ kātaliyai maṇattaṟku avaḷ peṟṟōr icaiyāviṭiṉ, avaḷaip peṟutaṟku maṭalēṟṟam eṉṉum uyirccētattiṟ kiṭamāṉa oru vaṉmuṟaiyaik kaiyāḷva tuṇṭu. atu ikkālattuc cattiyākkirakameṉṉum pāṭukiṭappup pōṉṟatu.
maṭalēṟat tuṇinta kātalaṉ, nīrccīlai oṉṟēyuṭuttu uṭampelāñ cāmpaṟpūci erukkamālaiyaṇintu, taṉ kātaliyiṉ ūr naṭuvē tavanilaiyi lamarntu, avaḷ uruvai varainta oru tuṇiyaik kaiyilēnti, atai uṟṟunōkkiya vaṇṇamāy vāḷāviruppaṉ. ataṉaik kaṇṭa av vūrār nī āyvu (cōtaṉai) tarukiṉṟāyā? eṉak kēṭpar. avaṉ tarukiṉṟēṉ eṉiṉ, paṉaṅkarukku maṭṭaiyāṟ ceyta oru poyk kutiraiyiṉmēl avaṉaiyēṟṟit teru neṭuka iḻuttuc celvar. aṅṅaṉam iḻukkumpōtu, avaṉ uṭampiṟ karukkaṟuttuk kāyam paṭṭaviṭamellām veḷuttut tōṉṟiṉ, avaṉukku avaṉ kātaliyai maṇamuṭittu vaippar; allākkāl vaiyār. itu kātalar tāmākak kūṭuvataṉṟāyiṉum, orupuṭai yoppumaipaṟṟi iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭatu.
kātaṟ pāṭṭukaḷ
iv vulakiṟ ciṟantatu illaṟa iṉpam. atilum, kātalar nukarvatu karai yaṟṟatu. avar ōrūrarāyum oruvaraiyoruvar muṉṉaṟintavarāyum irukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum yāppuṟavillai. karuṅkaṭaluppiṟkum, karumalai nārattaikkum tontam ēṟpaṭuvatupōl, neṭṭiṭaippaṭṭa iruvar ōriṭattu oruvaraiyoruvar kaṇṭu kātalikkavum nērum. attakai nilaimai vāynta oru kātalaṉ kūṟṟāka vuḷḷa pāṭṭoṉṟu varumāṟu :
yāyum ñāyum yārā kiyarō
entaiyum nuntaiyum emmuṟaik kēḷir
yāṉum nīyum evvaḻi aṟitum
cempulap peyaṉīr pōla
aṉpuṭai neñcam tāṅkalan taṉavē.	(kuṟun. 40)
(yāy - eṉ tāy, ñāy - uṉ tāy. entai - eṉ tantai. nuntai - uṉ tantai. kēḷir - uṟaviṉar. cempulappeyalnīr pōla - civanta nilattiṟ cērnta nīrum civappatu pōla.)
iṉi, oru kātali kūṟṟāka vuḷḷa pāṭṭoṉṟu varumāṟu :
irāmaḻai peyta īral īrattuḷ
paṉainukaṅ koṇṭu yāṉaiyēr pūṭṭi
veḷḷi viraittup poṉṉē viḷaiyiṉum
vēṇṭēṉ piṟantakat tīṇṭiya vāḻvē
ceṅkēḻ varaku pacuṅkatir koytu
kaṉṟu kāttuk kuṉṟil uṇakki
ūṭupatar pōkkimuṉ utaviṉōrkku kutavik
kāṭukaḻi intaṉam pāṭupārt teṭuttuk
kuppaik kīrai uppiṉṟi ventatai
iraval tālam parivuṭaṉ vāṅkic
cōṟatu koṇṭu pīṟal aṭaittē
oṉṟuviṭ ṭorunāḷ tiṉṟu kiṭappiṉum
naṉṟē tōḻinam kaṇavaṉ vāḻvē.
(nukam-nukattaṭi. īṇṭiya-celvamikunta. uṇakki-kāyavaittu. intaṉam-viṟaku. pāṭu-viḻutal. tālam-uṇkalam. parivu-varuttam.pīṟal-cēlaikkiḻivu).
(3) kavarvu maṇam
kavarvu maṇamāvatu, oru peṇṇaip peṟṟōricaivum avaḷ icaivumiṉṟi, valintu paṟṟutal.
atu, ūrukku veḷiyē taṉittu niṟkum ōr iḷampeṇṇai, vēṟṟūrāṉ oruvaṉ valintu paṟṟikkoṇṭu pōy, avaḷai maṉaiviyākak koḷvatu pōṉṟatu.
oru maṟakkuṭip peṇṇai maṇakka virumpiya vēntaṉ, ak kuṭiyār ataṟkicaiyāviṭattu, perum paṭaiyoṭu ceṉṟu avarōṭu pōr purivatuṇṭu. am maṟavar tam kuṭiyiṉ māṉattaik kāttaṟku añcātu etirppar. itu makaṭpāṟ kāñci eṉappaṭum.
nikarttumēl vanta vēntaṉoṭu mutukuṭi
makaṭpā ṭañciya makaṭpā vāṉum	(puṟattiṇaiyiyal, 24) 
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyatu ituvē. itaṉaiyē, maṟam eṉṉuṅ kalampaka vuṟuppu, aracaṉ peṇkēṭṭu viṭutta tirumukattaik koṇarnta tūtaṉai nōkki, maṟavar eccarittum accuṟuttiyum viṭuppatākak kūṟum.
oru maṇappeṇṇiṉ aḻakaip paḻikkumpōtu, avaḷ eṉṉa oru periya ciṟaiyā? eṉṟu peṇṭir kūṟum vaḻakkam, oru kālattiṟ pēraḻakuṭaiya peṇkaḷ valiyōrālum aracālum ciṟaipiṭikkappaṭṭamaiyai uṇarttum.
ōr aracaṉ, taṉ pakaiyaracaṉai veṉṟu allatu koṉṟu, avaṉuṭaiya tēviyaraic ciṟaipiṭittu vantu vēḷam eṉṉum ciṟaiccālaiyil iṭṭu avamāṉappaṭuttuvatu, kavarvu maṇattiṉ pāṟpaṭātu.
2. kulamuṟai paṟṟiyatu
paṇṭaimuṟait tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ, kulamuṟaipaṟṟi akamaṇam (Endogamy), puṟamaṇam (Exogamy) eṉa iruvakaippaṭum.
akamaṇamāvatu, oru kulattār tam kulattiṟkuḷḷēyē maṇattal; puṟamaṇamāvatu oru kulap pirivār tam piriviṟkuḷ maṇavātu vēṟoru pirivil maṇattal.
iṉṟuḷḷa kulaṅkaḷuḷ, kalappuk kulaṅkaḷ tavira ēṉaiya vellām akamaṇattaṉavē. tiṇaimayakkam ēṟpaṭu muṉ kuṟavar, āyar, vēṭṭuvar, uḻavar, nuḷaiyar (cempaṭavar) eṉat tamiḻar aintiṇai makkaḷāy vevvēṟu nilattil vāḻntapōtu, avarkkuḷ perumpāl vaḻakkamāy nikaḻntatu akamaṇamē.
kulappirivukaḷ nāṭu, ūr, kulam, kūṭṭam, kiḷai, vakuppu, illam, karai mutaliyaṉavākap palvēṟu tiṟappaṭum.
nāṭum ūrum illum kuṭiyum
piṟappum ciṟappum	(1060)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
cēlam māvaṭṭattuc cērvarāyaṉmalai, paccaimalai, kollimalai mutaliya malaikaḷil vāḻum tamiḻ malaiyāḷik kulattil, (1) periya malaiyāḷi, (2) paccai malaiyāḷi, (3) kolli malaiyāḷi eṉṉum mūṉṟum uṭkulaṅkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ, kolli malaiyāḷi eṉṉum uṭkulattil (1) munnāṭṭu malaiyāḷi, (2) nālnāṭṭumalaiyāḷi, (3) añcūr malaiyāḷi eṉṉum nāṭṭut tokutiyil, (1) mayilam, (2) tiruppuli, (3) iṭappuli, (4) piṟakarai, (5) ciṟṟūr eṉṉum aintum nāṭukaḷ eṉṉum uṭpirivukaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ, ciṟṟūr eṉṉum nāṭṭil, (1) pīlaṉ, (2) mūkkāṇṭi, (3) pūcaṉ, (4) māṇikkaṉ, (5) tiruvicci, (6) kaṇṇaṉ, (7) tillāṉ eṉṉum ēḻum vakuppukaḷ eṉṉum uṭpirivukaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉ aintum oru tokutit tāyāti vakuppukaḷ; piṉ iraṇṭum maṟṟoru tokutit tāyāti vakuppukaḷ. iv iru tokutikaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟum aṭutta tokutiyuḷaṉṟit taṉ tokutiyuḷ maṇappatillai. itu puṟamaṇamām.
koṅkuvēḷāḷar kulattiṉ uṭpirivukaḷāṉa, tūraṅkulam cempōttaṅ kulam, āntaikkulam mutaliya irupattiraṇṭu kulaṅkaḷum, puṟamaṇattaṉavām. ik kulaṅkaḷ kūṭṭameṉavumpaṭum.
orē kulattuḷ toṭarntu maṇaṅkaḷ naṭaipeṟuvatāl viḷaiyum kēṭṭai vilakkutaṟkē, kulappirivukaṭkup puṟamaṇam vitikkappaṭṭatākat teri kiṉṟatu.
aracar piṟakulattil tām peṇkoṇṭālum, tam makaḷiraip piṟakulattārkkuk koṭuppatillai. itu uyarmaṇattiṉ (Hypergamy) pāṟpaṭum.
3. maṇamakkaḷ tokaipaṟṟiyatu
maṇamakkaḷ tokaipaṟṟi, tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ (1) oru maṉaiyam (Monogamy), (2) palmaṉaiyam (Polygamy), (3) palkaṇavam (Polyandry) eṉa mūvakaippaṭum.
oru kālattil orē maṉaiviyuṭaimai orumaṉaiyam; orē kālattil pala maṉaiviyaruṭaimai palmaṉaiyam; orē kālattil pala kaṇavaruṭaimai palkaṇavam.
paṇṭaik kālattil, pala aracarum talaivarum, palmaṉaiyattait taḻuviyatuṭaṉ potumakaḷirākiya parattaiyaruṭaṉum toṭarpu koṇ ṭiruntaṉar. parattaiyaruḷ, vēṟāka ōr illattil iruppavaḷ iṟparattai yeṉṟum, parattaiyar cēriyil iruppavaḷ cēripparattaiyeṉṟum, cēripparattaiyaruḷ ciṟantu kātalikkappaṭupavaḷ kātaṟparattaiyeṉṟum collappaṭuvar.
parattaiyar pōṉṟē potumakaḷirāyuḷḷa iṉṉoru vakuppār kaṇikaiyar (kūttiyar) eṉpār. kālaṅkaṇittāṭupavar kaṇikaiyar. avar nāṭakak kaṇikaiyar kōyiṟ kaṇikaiyar eṉa irucārār.
iṉi, potumakaḷirallātu, karaṇamiṉṟi maṉaiviyarpōl vaittuk koḷḷappaṭum vaippu eṉṉum makaḷirumuṇṭu. avar kaṉṉiyarākavō kaṭṭuppaṭṭavarākavō kaimpeṇṭirākavō irukkalām.
paṇṭaittamiḻar potuvākap paṇpāṭṭilum paltuṟaippaṭṭa nākarikattilum ciṟantiruntārēṉum, peṇṇiṉpattuṟaiyil perumpālum nekiḻnta neṟiyuṭaiyarākavē yiruntaṉar. aracar kaṭṭumaṭṭiṉṟic ciṟṟiṉpattai nukarntu vantatiṉālēyē, ulā, maṭal, kātal mutaliya akapporuṭ paṉuvalkaḷum (pirapantaṅkaḷ) parattaiyiṟ pirivu eṉṉum kōvaik kiḷavikkottum iṭaikkālat teḻuntaṉa. mēṉāṭṭār in nāvalan tēyattiṟku vanta piṉpē, in neṟitiṟampiya nilai tiruntat toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
palkaṇavam peṇ pañcattālum peṇkolaiyālum ēṟpaṭuvatu. atu iṉṟu nīlamalait totuvariṭaiyē uḷḷatu.
4. maṇamakaḷnilai paṟṟiyatu
maṇamakaḷ nilaipaṟṟi, tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ. (1) kaṉṉi maṇam, (2) kaṭṭuppaṭṭavaḷ maṇam, (3) kaimpeṇ maṇam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.
kaṉṉi maṇam, oru kaṉṉip peṇṇaip putitāka maṇattal; kaṭṭup paṭṭavaḷ maṇam, oru muṟai maṇakkappaṭṭut tīrappaṭṭavaḷai maṇattal; kaimpeṇ maṇam, kaṇavaṉai yiḻantavaḷai maṇattal. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉavai yiraṇṭum iḻintōr maṇamākavum iṭaittarattōr maṇamākavum iruntuvantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
oru muṟai maṇakkappaṭṭavaḷai maṟumaṇañ ceytal ciṟappillāta tākak karutappaṭuvatāl, kaṭṭuppaṭṭavaḷ maṇattil maṇamuḻā (kaliyāṇa mēḷam) iṉṟiyē tāli kaṭṭappaṭuvatumuṇṭu. atu kaṭṭuttāliyeṉṟum, maṇamuḻavu uḷḷatu koṭṭuttirumaṇam eṉṟum peyar peṟum. kaimpeṇ maṇam perumpālum kaṭṭuttāliyākavē yirukkum.
(2) ilakkaṇap pākupāṭu
iṉi, tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ, maṇamakkaḷiṉ kātalnilaipaṟṟit tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷai, (1) kaikkiḷai, (2) aṉpiṉaintiṇai, (3) peruntiṇai eṉa mūvakaiyāka vakuttuk kūṟum.
kaikkiḷaiyāvatu, āṭavaṉ peṇṭu ākiya iruvaruḷḷum oruvarukkē kātal iruppatu; aṉpiṉ aintiṇaiyāvatu, av iruvarukkum kātal iruppatu; peruntiṇaiyāvatu, av iruvarukkum kātal illā tiruppatu allatu oruvaraiyoruvar valintukoḷvatu. im mūṉṟum, muṟaiyē orutalaik kāmam, irutalaik kāmam, poruntāk kāmam eṉṟum collappeṟum. kai eṉpatu pakkam. kiḷai eṉpatu nēyam. ākavē kaikkiḷai eṉpatu orutalaik kātal. aintiṇai eṉpaṉa, kuṟiñci, mullai, pālai, marutam, neytal eṉṉum ainnilaṅkaḷ. kuṟiñci malaiyum malaicārnta iṭamum; mullai malaiyaṟṟa ceṭiyuṅkāṭum pulveḷiyum; pālai nīrum niḻalumiṉṟi vaṟṟi vaṟaṇṭa iṭam; marutam nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamum oruṅkē yuḷḷa vayalnāṭu; neytal kaṭalum kaṭaṟkaraiyum. iv ainnilaṅkaṭkum potuvākak koḷḷappaṭṭatiṉāl, irutalaik kātal aṉpiṉ aintiṇai yeṉappaṭṭatu.
peruntiṇai perumpakuti makkaḷ maṇamuṟaikaḷum perumpālaṉa vaṟṟait  taṉṉuḷ aṭakki niṟpatāl, poruntāk kāmam peruntiṇai yeṉap paṭṭatu. kavarvu maṇaṅkaḷum, iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉa ellāp puṇarcci vakaikaḷum, peruntiṇaiyē. kātaloṭu poruntāmaiyum neṟiyoṭu poruntāmaiyumpaṟṟi, peruntiṇai poruntāk kāmam eṉappaṭṭatu.
kaikkiḷaikkum peruntiṇaikkum nilamillātapaṭi, aivakai nilaṅkaḷaiyum naṭunāyakamāṉa aṉpiṉ aintiṇaikkē urimaiyākki viṭṭataṉāl, makkaṭku nalam payavāta ēṉai yirumaṇa muṟaikaḷum in nilavulakattillātu nīṅkal vēṇṭum eṉpatu, muṉṉait tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlāriṉ karuttākum.
maṇamakkaḷiṉ icaivaik kēḷātu peṟṟōrē muṭittu vaikkum tirumaṇaṅkaḷil, maṇamakaṉukku allatu maṇamakaḷukku maṭṭum kātal iruppiṉ, atu kaikkiḷai maṇamām; iruvarukkum iruppiṉ, atu aṉpiṉ aintiṇaiyām; oruvarukkum illāviṭiṉ, peruntiṇaiyiṉ pāṟpaṭuvatām.
maṇamakkaḷ iruvaruḷ oruvarukku maṭṭum kātal irukkumpōtu, ēṉaiyavarum etirppiṉṟi icaintiruntāltāṉ, atu kaikkiḷai maṇamākum. allākkāl atu peruntiṇaiyē. orutalaik kāmam eṉpatu kātaliṉṟiyum icaintuvarum peṇṇai āṭavaṉum āṭavaṉaip peṇṇum maṇappatē yaṉṟi, icaiyāp peṇṇaiyum āṭavaṉaiyum valitil maṇappataṉṟu.
orutalaik kāmam eṉṉum muṟaiyil kaikkiḷaiyum, peruntiṇaiyum okkumēṉum, muṉṉatu neṟippaṭṭateṉṟum, piṉṉatu neṟitiṟampiyateṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
kāmavuṇarcciyillāc ciṟumiyiṭattum, kāmavuṇarcciyiruntum taṉmēṟ kātal koḷḷāp paruvappeṇṇiṭattum, oruvaṉ kātal moḻikaḷaik kūṟi iṉpuṟuvatu kaikkiḷaik kuṟippēyaṉṟik kaikkiḷai maṇamākātu.
irutalaiyuṅ kātaluḷḷaviṭattum, mutiyāḷoṭu kūṭutalum, kūṭṭa iṭaiyīṭupāṭṭāl ēṟpaṭum kāmap pittamum kaḻineñcaḻivum, peruntiṇai yeṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl; viṇṇiṉpattai yottatoru peṇ ṇiṉpattai maṇṇulakōrkku vakuttaṉar muṉṉait tamiḻ ilakkaṇiyar eṉka.
ittakaiya iṉpam mikamika aritāy orucilarkkē kiṭṭuvatāyiṉum, atai ōr aḷavaiyākakkoṇṭu kūṟiyatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam eṉappaṭum.
2. maṇattokai
paṇṭait tamiḻ maṇaṅkaṭku ittuṇai eṉṉun tokai varampillai. maṇapparuvam maṇamakaṉukkup patiṉāṟāṇṭeṉṟum, maṇamakaḷukkup paṉṉīrāṇṭeṉṟum, nūlkaḷ kūṟum. paṇṭaik kālat tamiḻmakkaḷ ettuṇai valimai mikkavarāy iruntiruppiṉum, nūlkaḷil maṇamakkaṭkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa paruvam muntiyatē. akkālattil tirumaṇac caṭṭa varampiṉmaiyālum, iṉpac ciṟappoṉṟaiyē makkaḷ karutiyatālum, iḷamai mutal mutumaivarai ettuṇai maṇañceyya muṭiyumō attuṇai maṇañ ceytaṟku aṉṟu iṭamiruntatu. āyiṉum cila aracarum kīḻōrumē in nilaiyai mikutiyāyp payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. peṇṭirkkuk kāmanukarccip paruvam paṉṉīrāṇṭu mutal nāṟpatāṇṭuvarai eṉpatu nūṉmarapu.
peṇṇiṉ aintāṇṭu mutal nāṟpatāṇṭu varaippaṭṭa paruvattaip piṉvarumāṟu eḻunilaiyāka ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ vakuttukkūṟum.
(1) pētai	 5- 7āṇṭu	(5) arivai	20-25 āṇṭu
(2) petumpai	 8-11 āṇṭu	(6) terivai	26-31 āṇṭu.
(3) maṅkai	12-13 āṇṭu	(7) pēriḷampeṇ	32-40 āṇṭu
(4) maṭantai	14-19 āṇṭu
aracarkkuc celvacciṟappu, kīḻōrukkut tīrvai muṟaiyum maṉaiviyar uḻaippum, palmaṇañ ceytaṟkut tuṇaiyāyiruntaṉa.
akkālat taracar kaṇkaṇṭa teyvaṅkaḷātaliṉ, avar paṉmaṇañ ceytalait taṭuppār evarumilar. ātalāl, avar vaḻimuṟai  maṇaṅkaṭkuk kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭa vēṇṭuvatillai. kīḻōr, nōyum mātappūppum caṇṭaiyum
pōlac ciṟu kūṭṭattaṭai ēṟpaṭiṉum, ataik kāraṇamākak kāṭṭi maṟumaṇañ ceytukoḷvar. mutal maṉaivikku ōrāṇṭu piḷḷaiyillā viṭiṉ, uṭaṉē maṟumaṇañ ceytukoḷvatu cilar iyalpu. cilar, piḷḷai yiruppiṉum, irupālum illaiyeṉṟum palarillaiyeṉṟum collik koṇṭu, maṟumaṇañ ceyvatuṇṭu. itu iṉṟum toṭarntu varukiṉṟatu.
3. maṇa naṭaimuṟai
paṇṭait tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu naṭantu vantaṉa:
(1) maṇappēccu
potuvāka, ōr iḷaiñaṉukkup patiṉeṭṭāṇṭu nirampiya piṉ, avaṉ peṟṟōr avaṉ kāmak kuṟippaṟintō tāmākak karutiyō, perumpālum marapuppaṭi otta kulattil oru peṇṇaip pēci maṇañ ceytuvaippar.
ammāṉ makaḷum attai makaḷumpōla muṟaikārap peṇṇāyiṉ `urimaippeṇ' eṉṟum, vēṟu celvar vīṭṭup peṇṇāyiṉ `perumaip peṇ' eṉṟum aḻaippatu vaḻakkam. urimaip peṇ illāviṭattum avaḷai maṇavāviṭattum, ayalilēyē maṇam pēcap peṟum.
maṇappēccu muṉkālattil oru tollaiyāṉa kalaiyāka iruntu
vantatu. maṇamakaṉ peṟṟōr, maṇakkat takka oru peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōriṭam, vaḷḷuvaṉ allatu kaṇiyaṉ kuṟitta naṉṉāḷil, periyōrai viṭuppar. peṟṟōrum uṭaṉ celvatuṇṭu. peṇ pēcac cellum pōtu, kuṟi (cakuṉam), naṟcol (vāyppuḷ), puḷ (paṟavainimittam) mutaliyaṉa pārttal vaḻakkam.
peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr tam makaḷait tara icaintapiṉ maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭār maṇamakaṉukkum peṇṇukkumuḷḷa poruttaṅkaḷaik kaṇiya (cōtiṭa) muṟaippaṭi palvēṟu vakaiyiṟ pārppar.
apporuttaṅkaḷ, potuvāka,
(1) nāḷ (naṭcattiram)	(6) ōrai (irāci)
(2) kaṇam	(7) kōḷ (irāciyatipati)
(3) eṇṇikkai (mākēntiram)	(8) vaciyam
(4) peṇ nīṭci (stirī tīrkkam)	(9) iṇakkam (vētai)
(5) piṟavi (yōṉi)	(10) caraṭu (iraccu)
eṉap pattu vakaippaṭum. ip pattu vakaiyum oruṅkē perumpālum poruntuvatillai. ataṉāl, palvēṟu peṇṇai nāṭip palvēṟu ūrkkuc celvatu vaḻakkam. itu mikunta alaiccalaiyum kālak kaṭappaiyum uṇṭu paṇṇum. itaṉālēyē, oru maṇattiṟku ēḻu cerupput tēyavēṇṭum eṉṉum paḻamoḻi eḻuntatu. iṉi, mēṟkūṟiya patiṉ poruttattuṭaṉ, vāḻnāḷ, kuṟippu (pāvakam), maram, puḷ, kulam eṉṉum aimporuttam pārppatumuṇṭu.
(2) maṇavuṟuti (niccayārttam) 
yārēṉum oru peṇṇiṭam patiṉ poruttamum amaintiruppiṉ, allatu amaintiruppatākac collappaṭiṉ, uṟuppuk kuṟaiyum nōyk kuṟṟamum illāviṭattu, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭārum peṇvīṭṭārum oru naṉṉāḷil peṇvīṭṭil palaraṟiya maṇavuṟuti ceytukoḷvar. av vuṟutic caṭaṅkai uṟuti veṟṟilai (niccaya tāmpūlam) eṉṟum aḻaikkalām.
maṇavuṟutic caṭaṅkil, veṟṟilaipākku, kuṅkumam, mañcaḷ mutaliyaṉa vaikkappaṭṭa oru taṭṭil, peṇvīṭṭār kēṭṭa paricappaṇamum, peṇṇiṟku oru kūṟaiyum vaikkappeṟum. iru vīṭṭārum maṇappeṇṇiṟku aṇiyavēṇṭiya aṇikaḷum, tirumaṇac celavil ēṟṟukkoḷḷavēṇṭiya pakutikaḷum, aṉṟē pēci muṭivu ceyyappaṭum.
cila camayaṅkaḷil, maṇamakkaḷ kuṟṟaṅkuṟaikaḷai maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭārum, maṇamakaḷ kuṟṟaṅkuṟaikaḷai maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭārum, maṟaittuvaippatumuṇṭu. tirumaṇam iruvarkku vāḻvai yuṇṭupaṇṇum maṅkalanikaḻcci yātalāl, aṅṅaṉam maṟaittuvaippōr āyiram poy colli oru tirumaṇam ceytu vai (oru viḷakkēṟṟi vai) eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyaic collit tammait tēṟṟikkoḷvar.
(3) maṇaviḻā 
i. muṉṉikaḻccikaḷ 
maṇavuṟutiyāṉa piṉ, maṇattiṟkuriya cittirai, āṉi pōṉṟa nalla mātattil, tiṅkaḷ, aṟivaṉ (putaṉ) pōṉṟa nalla kiḻamaiyil, 2āvatu 7āvatu pōṉṟa nalla pakkattil (titiyil), puravi (acupati), cakaṭu (urōkaṇi) pōṉṟa nalla nāḷil (naṭcattirattil), nalla ōraiyil (ilakkiṉattil) amaiyum maṅkala muḻuttam (mukūrttam) kuṟikkap peṟum. ataṉpiṉ, maṇacceyti kālamum iṭamuṅ kuṟittu, uṟṟār uṟaviṉar aṉaivarkkum pākku vaittuc collappeṟum.
maṇamaṉai, kulamarapuppaṭi, maṇamakaṉ vīṭākavō maṇamakaḷ vīṭākavō irukkum. potuvāy, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭil maṇam naṭaipeṟum.
maṇamaṉaiyil, maṇanāḷukku muṉ 3, 5, 7, 9 ākiya oṟṟitta nāḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟil, nalvēḷaiyil muḻuttakkāl nāṭṭip pantal allatu koṭṭakai pōṭappeṟum. pantal alaṅkarippu avaravar celva nilaiyaip poṟuttatu. pantalil vāḻai kamuku kūntaṟpaṉaimaṭal mutaliyaṉa kaṭṭappeṟuvatuṇṭu. ettuṇai yeḷiyarāyiṉum vāḻaimaram tappātu kaṭṭappeṟum. atu maṇamakkaḷ marapu kuñcuṅ kuḷuvāṉumāy vaḻivaḻi vāḻavēṇṭum eṉṉuṅ kuṟippaiyuṭaiyatākak karutappeṟuvatu.
pantal naṭuvil allatu ataṉ oru kōṭiyil maṇavaṟai amaikkap paṭum. ataṉ kāloṉṟil aracaṅkompu kaṭṭappeṟum. atai aracāṇik
kāl eṉpar. paṇṭaikkālattil aracaṉ teyvamākak karutappaṭṭatiṉālum, maṉṉaṉ uyirttē malartalai yulakam eṉpatiṉālum, aracaṉaik kuṟittaṟku aracaṅkompai maṇavaṟaiyil naṭṭi, atai araca āṇikkāl eṉṟu aḻaittatākat terikiṉṟatu. maṇavaṟaiyai aracāṇi mēṭai eṉpatumuṇṭu.
maṇavaṟaiyil pū, mañcaḷ, kuṅkumam, cantaṉam, arici, aṟuku, veṟṟilaipākku, tēṅkāy, muḷaippālikai, viḷakku, niṟaikumpam, kōlappāṉai mutaliya maṅkalap poruḷkaḷ vaikkappeṟum. kōlap pāṉaiyai āyiraṅkaṇṇup pāṉaiyeṉṟum, aracāṇip pāṉaiyeṉṟum āyirattāḻiyeṉṟuñ colvatuṇṭu. maṇamakkaḷ kuṟaiviṉṟi maṅkalamāy nīṭūḻi vāḻavēṇṭumeṉpatē, maṇavaṟaiyil maṅkalap poruḷkaḷai vaippatiṉ karuttu. viḷakkut teyvac ciṉṉam.
maṇaviṉaikaḷ toṭaṅkumuṉpē, maṇamakaṉukkum maṇamakaḷukkum, muṟaiyē, valakkaiyilum iṭakkaiyilum kāppunāṇ kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. maṇaviḻā muṭiyumvarai maṇamakkaṭkup pēyālum piṟavaṟṟālum evvakait tīṅkum nērakkūṭāteṉpatē, ataṉ nōkkam. kāppu tīṅku varāmaṟ kāttal. mutaṟkālattil kuḷicampōṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa kāppunāṇē, piṟkālattil ap peyaruḷḷa aṇiyāka māṟiṟṟu.
maṇanāḷil maṇamaṉaiyil maṅkala maṇamuḻā muḻaṅkum. uṟṟārum uṟaviṉarum, maṇappantaṟkīḻ vantamarvar. tirumaṇa āciriyaṉ muḻutta vēḷaiyil maṇavaṟaiyil vantamarvāṉ. avaṉ tamiḻap pārppāṉākavō, kulattalaivaṉākavō iruppāṉ. maṇamakkaḷum nīrāṭi mañcaḷ tōytta allatu tōyāta puttāṭai yaṇintu, maṇamakaṉ muṉṉum maṇamakaḷ piṉṉumāka, taṉittaṉiyāka maṇavaṟaikkuk koṇṭu varappaṭuvar. maṇamaṉai maṇamakaṉ vīṭāyiṉ maṇamakaḷum, maṇamakaḷ vīṭāyiṉ maṇamakaṉum, vēṟōr maṉaiyiliruntu mēḷa tāḷattuṭaṉ aḻaittuvarappeṟuvar.
ii. karaṇam 
tirumaṇa āciriyaṉ teyva vaḻipāṭāṟṟi, maṇamakkaḷ peyarum maṇamum viḷampi, viḷakkuc cāṉṟāka avarkaḷaic cūḷiṭuvittu, kuṅkumam mañcaḷuṭaṉ taṭṭiliṭṭuc cūṭaṅ koḷuttappaṭṭup periyōrāl vāḻttap peṟṟa tāliyai eṭuttu, maṇamakaṉ kaiyil koṭukka, avaṉ atai vāṅkip peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil kaṭṭuvaṉ. aṉṟu muḻavu iṭiyeṉa muḻaṅkum. maṇamakkaḷmēl malarmāri poḻiyum. ataṉpiṉ maṇamakkaḷ mālai māṟṟikkoḷvar. periyōr aṟukum ariciyum maṇamakkaḷmēl iṭṭu attipōl tuḷirttu, ālpōṟ paṭarntu (taḻaittu), aracupōl ōṅki, aṟukupōl vērūṉṟi, mūṅkilpōṟ cuṟṟam muciyāmal vāḻntiruppīr eṉṟum, `pakavaṉaruḷāl pāṅkāyiruntu patiṉāṟum peṟṟup pallāṇṭu vāḻkaeṉṟum, piṟavāṟum vāḻttuvar. itu `aṟukiṭal eṉappaṭum.
iii. piṉṉikaḻccikaḷ 
tirumaṇattiṟku vantōrkkellām tēṅkāy paḻamum pākku veṟṟilaiyum cantaṉamum pakirappeṟum. maṅkala makaḷirum ciṟumiyarum avaṟṟoṭu malarum peṟuvar. ataṉpiṉ tirumaṇa avai kalaiyum. āyiṉ, uṟṟār uṟaviṉarellām aṅkēyē yiruntu maṇavīṭṭārkku moyyum maṇamakkaṭku naṉkoṭaiyum vaḻaṅkuvar. moyyellām ōlaiyil eḻutappeṟum. maṇamakkaḷ periyōr kālil viḻuntu vaṇaṅkuvar.
aṉṟu mālai allatu iravu, ūrvalam nikaḻum. maṇamakkaḷ avvak kula marapuppaṭi, tērilō, pallakkilō, yāṉai kutirai mītō valam varuvar. ūrvalam muṭintu vīṭu cērntavuṭaṉ, tēṅkāyuṭaittum, mañcaḷ nīrāl maṇamakkaḷ pātaṅkaḷaik kaḻuviyum, ālatti yeṭuttum, kaṇṇeccil (kaṇtiruṣṭi) kaḻikkappaṭum.
tirumaṇa viruntu akkālattil vēḷaikkaṇakkā yirātu, nāṭkaṇakkā yirukkum. eḷiyar oru nāḷum, celvar ēḻunāḷ varaiyum viruntaḷippar.
maṇamakkaḷ paruvam vantōrāyiṉ aṉṟē kūṭṭappeṟuvar.
maṇaviḻā muṭintavuṭaṉ, maṇamakkaṭkuk kāppuk kaḻaṟṟappaṭum.
maṇamāṉapiṉ, 3ām, 5ām, 7ām, 9ām nāḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟil, maṇamakkaḷ, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭil maṇam naṭantāl maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭiṟkum, maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭil maṇam naṭantāl maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭiṟkumāka vīṭu māṟuvar. itu maṟuvīṭu (allatu maruvīṭu) pōtal eṉappaṭum. maṟuvīṭu maṇamakaḷ vīṭāyiṉ, aṅku maṇamakaṉukkuc ceyyappaṭum viruntu, maru allatu maruvu eṉap peyar peṟum.
maṉaiyaṟam 
maṇaviṉaikaḷellām muṭintapiṉ, kōvalaṉ kaṇṇaki pōlum celvakkuṭi maṇamakkaḷai illaṟattiṟkuriya poruḷkaḷellām iṭṭu nirappappeṟṟa oru taṉimaṉaiyil iruttuvatu marapu. atu maṉaiyaṟam paṭuttal eṉappaṭum.
ciṉṉāṭ ceṉṟapiṉ, maṇamakaḷ peṟṟōr maṇamakaṉ vīṭu ceṉṟu, tam makaḷ maṉaiyaṟam naṭattum tiṟattaip pārvaiyiṭuvatuṇṭu. atu vīṭu pārttal eṉappaṭum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyaṉa ellārkkum potuvāṉa paruviṉaic caṭaṅkukaḷ. iṉi, avvak kulamarapiṟkēṟpac ciṟappāka naṭaipeṟum nuṇviṉaic caṭaṅkukaḷ ettaṉaiyō pala. virivañci avai viṭukkappaṭṭuḷa.
paṇṭaikkāla vatuvaimaṇam paṟṟiya iru pāṭṭukaḷ varumāṟu:
vāyil maṟutta tōḻikkut talaimakaṉ colliyatu.
uḻuntutalaip peyta koḻuṅkaḷi mitavai
peruñcōṟ ṟamalai niṟpa niraikāl
taṇperum pantart tarumaṇal ñemiri
maṉaiviḷak kuṟuttu mālai toṭarik 
kaṉaiyiruḷ akaṉṟa kaviṉpeṟu kālaik	(5)
kōḷkāl nīṅkiya koṭuveṇ ṭiṅkaḷ
kēṭil viḻuppukaḻ nāḷtalai vanteṉa
uccik kuṭattar puttakal maṇṭaiyar
potucey kampalai mutucem peṇṭir
muṉṉavum piṉṉavum muṟaimuṟai tarattarap	(10)
putalvaṟ payanta titalaiyav vayiṟṟu
vāliḻai makaḷir nālvar kūṭik
kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala utavip
peṟṟōṟ peṭkum piṇaiyai yākeṉa
nīroṭu corinta īritaḻ alari	(15)
palliruṅ katuppiṉ nelloṭu tayaṅka
vatuvai naṉmaṇaṅ kaḻinta piṉṟaik
kalleṉ cummaiyar ñerēreṉap pukutantu
pēriṟ kiḻatti yākeṉat tamartara
ōriṟ kūṭiya uṭaṉpuṇar kaṅkul	(20)
koṭumpuṟam vaḷaiik kōṭik kaliṅkat
toṭuṅkiṉaḷ kiṭanta ōrpuṟan taḻīi
muyaṅkal viruppoṭu mukamputai tiṟappa
añciṉaḷ uyirtta kālai yāḻaniṉ
neñcam paṭarnta teñcā turaiyeṉa	(25)
iṉṉakai irukkaip piṉyāṉ viṉavaliṉ
ceñcūṭ ṭoṇkuḻai vaṇkātu tuyalvara
akamali uvakaiyaḷ āki mukaṉikut
toyyeṉa iṟaiñci yōḷē māviṉ
maṭaṅkoḷ mataiiya nōkkiṉ	(30)
oṭuṅkīr ōti māa yōḷē.	(akam. 86)   
itaṉ poruḷ
1-4. uḻuttam paruppoṭu cērttuc camaitta koḻumaiyāṉa    kuḻainta poṅkalōṭu periya cōṟṟuttiraḷaiyai uṇṭal iṭaiyaṟātu nikaḻa, varicaiyāṉa kālkaḷaiyuṭaiya kuḷirnta periya pantaṟkīḻk koṇṭuvantu koṭṭiya maṇalaip parappi vīṭṭil viḷakkēṟṟi, mālaikaḷait toṅkaviṭṭu.
5-10. tīya kōḷkaḷiṉ toṭarpu nīṅkiya vaḷainta veṇṇilāvaik kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟanta pukaḻaiyuṭaiya cakaṭam (urōkaṇi) eṉṉum nāḷ aṭaiya, mikunta iruḷ nīṅkiya aḻaku poruntiya viṭiyaṟkālaiyil, uccantalaiyiṟ kuṭattaiyum kaiyil putiya akaṉṟa montaiyaiyum uṭaiya, maṇañceytu vaikkum āravāramuḷḷa mutiya maṅkala makaḷir muṉṉē taruvaṉavaṟṟaiyum piṉṉē taruvaṉavaṟṟaiyum muṟaippaṭi eṭutteṭuttuk koṭukka, 
11-16. makaṉaip peṟṟa tēmaluḷḷa aḻakiya vayiṟṟiṉaiyum tūya aṇikaḷaiyum uṭaiya makaḷir nālvar kūṭi niṉṟu, kaṟpiṉiṉṟum tavaṟātu pala naṟpēṟukaḷaippeṟṟu, uṉ kaṇavaṉ virumpip pēṇum viruppattiṟ kiṭamākuka eṉṟu vāḻtti, nīrōṭu cērttup peyta kuḷirnta itaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya pūkkaḷ, aṭarnta kariyakūntalil nellōṭu viḷaṅka,
17. nalla vatuvai maṇam muṭinta piṉpu.
18-20. cuṟṟattār āravāra ōcaiyuṭaṉ viraintu vantu, periya maṉaikkiḻattiyāvāy eṉṟu collic cērttu vaikka, ōr aṟaiyil uṭaṉ kūṭiya puṇarccikkuriya iravil,
21-26. mutukai vaḷaittuk kōṭip puṭavaikkuḷ oṭuṅkik kiṭanta pakkattaic cārntu, kaṭṭiyaṇaikkum viruppattuṭaṉ mukattai mūṭiyirunta āṭaiyai vilakka, avaḷ accattōṭu mūccuviṭṭapōtu, uṉ uḷḷam niṉaittatai oḷiyātu col eṉṟu piṉpu yāṉ kēṭṭatiṉāl, iṉiya makiḻcciyōṭu irukkaiyil,
27-31. māṉiṉ maṭattōṭu cerukkaiyuṅkoṇṭa pārvaiyaiyum oṭuṅkiya kuḷirnta kūntalaiyum uṭaiya, am māniṟattaiyuṭaiyavaḷ, civanta maṇikaḷ patitta viḷakkamāṉa tiraṇṭa kātaṇikaḷ kātil acaiya, uḷḷam niṟainta makiḻcciyaḷāki mukattait tāḻtti eṉṉai viraintu vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ.
2. uṇarppuvayiṉ vārā ūṭaṟkaṇ talaimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.
maippuṟap puḻukkiṉ neykaṉi veṇcōṟu
varaiyā vaṇmaiyoṭu puraiyōrp pēṇip
puḷḷuppuṇarn tiṉiya vākat teḷḷoḷi
aṅkaṇ iruvicumpu viḷaṅkat tiṅkaṭ
cakaṭam maṇṭiya tukaḷtīr kūṭṭattuk	(5)
kaṭinakar puṉaintu kaṭavuṭ pēṇip
paṭumaṇa muḻavoṭu parūuppaṇai yimiḻa 
vatuvai maṇṇiya makaḷir vituppuṟṟup
pūkkaṇum imaiyār nōkkupu maṟaiya
meṉpū vākaip puṉpuṟak kavaṭṭilai	(10)
paḻaṅkaṉṟu kaṟitta payampamal aṟukait
taḻaṅkukural vāṉiṉ talaippeyaṟ kīṉṟa
maṇṇumaṇi yaṉṉa māyitaḻp pāvait
taṇṇaṟu mukaiyoṭu veṇṇūl cūṭṭit
tūvuṭaip polintu mēvarat tuvaṉṟi	(15)
maḻaipaṭ ṭaṉṉa maṇaṉmali pantar
iḻaiyaṇi ciṟappiṟ peyarviyarp pāṟṟit
tamarnamak kītta talaināḷ iraviṉ
uvarnīṅku kaṟpiṉem uyiruṭam paṭuvi
muruṅkāk kaliṅkam muḻuvatum vaḷaiip	(20)
perumpuḻuk kuṟṟaniṉ piṟainutaṟ poṟiviyar
uṟuvaḷi yāṟṟac ciṟuvarai tiṟaveṉa
ārva neñcamoṭu pōrvai vavvaliṉ
uṟaikaḻi vāḷiṉ uruvupeyart timaippa
maṟaitiṟaṉ aṟiyā ḷāki oyyeṉa	(25)
nāṇiṉaḷ iṟaiñci yōḷē pēṇip
parūuppakai yāmpaṟ kurūuttoṭai nīlic
curumpimir āymalar vēynta
irumpal kūntal iruḷmaṟai yoḷittē'' 	               (akam. 136)
(i - ḷ.)
1-9. neñcē kuṟṟamaṟap paruppuṭaṉ kalantu ākkiya neymikunta veṇ cōṟṟai, nīṅkāta īkait taṉmaiyuṭaṉ uyarnta cuṟṟattār mutaliyōrai uṇpittu, puṭkuṟi iṉitākak kūṭa, teḷḷiya oḷiyaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya iṭamakaṉṟa periya vāṉam kaḷaṅkamaṟa viḷaṅka, tiṅkaḷaic cakaṭam kūṭiya kuṟṟamaṟṟa naṉṉāḷil, maṇamaṉaiyai aḻakupaṭuttik kaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭṭu, maṇa mēḷattuṭaṉ periya muracam muḻaṅka, talaivikku maṇanīrāṭṭiya makaḷir, tam kūriya kaṇkaḷāl imaiyātu nōkki viraintu maṟaiya,
10-18. melliya pūvaiyuṭaiya vākaiyiṉ aḻakaṟṟa piṉ puṟattaiyuṭaiya kavartta ilaiyai, mutiya kaṉṟu kaṟitta paḷḷattiṟ paṭarnta aṟukiṉ, iṭi muḻaṅkiya vāṉattu mutaṉ maḻaikku arumpiya kaḻuviya nīlamaṇi pōlum kariya itaḻaiyuṭaiya pāvaiyotta kiḻaṅkiṭattuḷḷa kuḷirnta maṇamuḷḷa arumpuṭaṉ, cērttukkaṭṭiya veḷḷiya nūlaiccūṭṭi, tūya puttāṭaiyāṟ poliyac ceytu, viruppattuṭaṉ kūṭi, maḻaiyōcai pōṉṟa maṇavōcai mikunta pantalil aṇikaḷai mikutiyāy aṇintiruntatiṉāl uṇṭāṉa viyarvaiyai viciṟiyāl āṟṟi, avaḷ cuṟṟattār avaḷai namakkut tanta mutal nāḷ iravil,
19-26. veṟuttalillāta kaṟpōṭukūṭi eṉ uyirukku uṭampāka aṭuttavaḷ, kacaṅkāta putuppōrvaiyait taṉ uṭal muḻutum pōrttik koṇṭatāl, mikunta puḻukkattaiyaṭainta uṉ piṟai pōṉṟa neṟṟiyil arumpiya viyarvaiyai, mikunta kāṟṟup pōkkumāṟu, ciṟitu tiṟa eṉṟu aṉpumikka neñcattōṭu pōrvaiyaik kavaravē, uṟaiyiṉiṉṟu uruviya vāḷaippōl avaḷ uruvam veḷippaṭṭu viḷaṅka, atai maṟaikkum vakai aṟiyātavaḷāki, viraintu nāṇi viruppattōṭu vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ.
attakaiya pēraṉpuṭaiyōḷ iṉṟu yām paṇimoḻi kūṟivēṇṭām uṇarātu ūṭukiṉṟaṉaḷ; ātalāl ivaḷ namakku yāraḷō?
kuṟippu : maippaṟap puḻukki eṉṟum pāṭam. puḻukki - avittu.
vāṉūr matiyañ cakaṭaṇaiya eṉṟu cilappatikārattum vantiruppatāl, tiṅkaḷaic cakaṭaṇaiyum nāḷ tirumaṇattiṟkuc ciṟanta naṉṉāḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.
muntiṉa pāṭṭiṟpōṉṟē itilum, tirumaṇanāḷaṉṟē maṇamakkaḷ kūṭṭappeṟṟār eṉṟu kuṟittiruppataik kavaṉikka.
II. iṭaikkāla māṟutalkaḷ 
pirāmaṇar peruntokaiyiṉarāyt tamiḻakam vantu cērnta piṉ, tamiḻar tirumaṇaṅkaḷiṟ pala māṟutalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
1. pirāmaṇap purōkitamum vaṭamoḻik karaṇamum 
pirāmaṇar tēvarvaḻi vanta nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉṟum; avar muṉṉōr moḻiyākiya vētamoḻiyum ataṉoṭu vētakāla intiya vaṭṭāramoḻikaḷākiya pirākirutaṅ kalanta ilakkiya moḻiyākiya camaṟ kirutamum, tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum; iru peruntavaṟāṉa karuttukaḷ kaṭaiccaṅka kālattilēyē aracar uḷḷattil āḻa vērūṉṟiviṭṭataṉāl, piṟkālattil pirāmaṇap purōkitam tamiḻakattil viraintu paravuvataṟku mikunta vacatiyāyiruntatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ palyākacālai mutukuṭumip peruvaḻutiyum, cōḻaṉ irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷiyum, cēraṉ palyāṉaic celkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉum pōṉṟa tamiḻ vēntarum piṟa ciṟṟaracarum, pirāmaṇarai nilattēvariṉum mēlākat tēvareṉṟēkoṇṭu, avar coṉṉatai yellām nampi avar ēviyataiyellām iyaṟṟi, vaṭamoḻi mantiram varaiyiṟanta valimaiyuḷḷateṉṟum, iṟaivaṉ cevikku ellaiyaṟṟa iṉpantaruvateṉṟum, āriya vēḷvikaḷellām tappātu oruvaṉai uyarkatikkuc celuttum eṉṟum, teyvattaṉmaiyuḷḷa vaṭamoḻi mantirattait teyvattaṉmaiyuḷḷa pirāmaṇaṉē ōtavallāṉeṉṟum, piṟar atai ōtiṉāl ataṉ āṟṟal keṭumeṉṟum, tamiḻaruḷ uyarntōrmaṭṭum ataik kātāṟ kēṭkavum ataṉāṟ payaṉpeṟavun takkavar eṉṟum, tamiḻmakkaḷ nampumāṟu ceytuviṭṭaṉar. maṉṉaṉ eppaṭi maṉṉuyir appaṭi aṉṟō!
pirāmaṇar mutalāvatu ciṟu tokaiyiṉarāyiruntatiṉāl, araca riṭattumaṭṭum āriyakkaraṇamum caṭaṅkum āṟṟivantaṉar. piṉpu caṟṟut tokaimikkapiṉ, peruñcelvarāṉa peru vaṇikarkkum āṟṟivantaṉar. ataṉ piṉ, tokai mikamikac ciṟuvaṇikarkkum uyar vēḷāḷarkkum āṟṟat talaippaṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, perumpālum ovvoru pērūrilum oru pirāmaṇaṉ kuṭiyamara allatu amarttappaṭa nērntatu. ōr ūrkkup potuvāyiruntu av vūriluḷḷa uyarntōr caṭaṅkukaḷaiyellām āṟṟi vanta pirāmaṇaṉ, ūrppārppāṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. pirāmaṇaṉ kuṭiyirāta ūrc caṭaṅkukaḷai, aṭuttavūrp pārppāṉ vantu ceyvatu vaḻakkam.
āriyak karaṇattāl viḷainta tīmaikaḷ
(1) tamiḻukkum tamiḻaṉukkum tāḻvu 
vaṭamoḻi vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyum caṭaṅku moḻiyumāy vaḻakkūṉ ṟiyapiṉ, tamiḻ avaṟṟiṟkut takātateṉṟu taḷḷappaṭṭut taṉ paḻan talaimaiyai iḻantatuṭaṉ, tāḻvum aṭaintatu. vaṭacoṟkaḷaic coṉṉāl uyarvum teṉcoṟkaḷaic coṉṉāl tāḻvum uṇṭākumeṉṟa tavaṟāṉa karuttu, tamiḻmakkaḷ uḷḷattiṟ pukuntatiṉāl, vaṭacoṟkaḷ ovvoṉṟāy vaḻakkiṟ pukuntu, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷai vaḻakku vīḻttiviṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻukku nērnta tāḻvu tamiḻaṉukkum nērntatu. ataṉāl, pirāmaṇaṉ toṭṭatai ellāt tamiḻarum uṇṇalāmeṉṟum, ettuṇai uyarnta tamiḻaṉāyiṉum, avaṉ toṭṭataip pirāmaṇaṉ uṇṇak kūṭāteṉṟum kūṭṭaravu (camutāya) ēṟpāṭukaḷ eḻuntaṉa. itu tamiḻaṉ poruḷiyal vāḻvai mikamikat tākkiṟṟu.
cila tūya tamiḻkkulattār, tamiḻaṉ uyarvaiyum varalāṟṟaiyum aṟiyātu, tammaic cattiriyareṉṟum vaiciyareṉṟum collit tamakku āriyat toṭarpu kōravum, cila uyar vēḷāḷar tammaic caṟcūttirar eṉṟaḻaittu, tammai uyarttuvatupōṟ karutikkoṇṭu uṇmaiyil tāḻttavum, nilaimai ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
tamiḻaruḷ uyarntavarākak karutappeṟum nellai maravūṇ (caiva) vēḷāḷarum pirāmaṇarukkuk kīḻppaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, tamiḻarellārum tāḻttappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉar. iṉṟu tāḻttappaṭṭavar eṉappaṭuvār uṇmaiyil oṭukkappaṭṭavarē.
(2) tamiḻap pārppārukkup piḻaippiṉmai 
pirāmaṇap purōkitar, uyarntōriṉ allatu uyarttappaṭṭōriṉ, ellā mataviyaṟ caṭaṅkuḷaiyum āṟṟum takutiyai muṟṟūṭṭākap peṟṟuviṭṭatiṉāl, tamiḻappārppār palarkkup piḻaippillātu pōyiṟṟu.
(3) ciṟuteyva vaḻipāṭu 
tamiḻaruḷ uyarntōr, ciṟuteyva vaṇakka nilaiyum perunteyva vaṇakka nilaiyum kaṭantu, muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷ vaṇakka nilaiyai aṭaintavar. aṅṅaṉamiruppavum, iṉṟu āriyak karaṇattāl katiravaṉ, tiṅkaḷ, varuṇaṉ, tī mutaliya iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷaiyum kōḷkaḷaiyum pūtaṅkaḷaiyum, vaṇaṅkum, muntiyal nilaimaikkuk koṇṭuvarappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉar.
(4) kaṟpiḻukkak kūṟṟu 
pirāmaṇap purōkitaṉ, maṇamakaṉai nōkki, nī mutal nāḷ tiṅka ḷukkum (cōmaṉukkum), iraṇṭām nāḷ yāḻōrukkum (kantaruvarukkum), mūṉṟām nāḷ tīkkum (akkiṉikkum) uṉ maṉaiviyai aḷittuviṭṭu nālām nāḷ avaḷai nukarvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟum karaṇavuraip pakuti,
teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ
peyyeṉap peyyum maḻai	(kuṟaḷ. 55)
eṉṟu koḷḷum tamiḻaṉ kaṟpuṇarccikku mikamika iḻukkuttaruva toṉṟām. atilum tiṅkaḷ nukarntuviṭṭu yāḻōrkkut tara, yāḻōr nukarntuviṭṭut tīkkut tara, tī nukarttuviṭṭu uṉakkut tara, nī avaḷaip peṟṟu nukarvāyāka eṉṟu maṇamakaḷmēṟ piṟarkku muṉṉurimaiyum maṇamakaṉukkup piṉṉurimaiyun tōṉṟak kūṟuvatu, evvakaiyiṉum ēṟṟukkoḷḷat takkataṉṟu.
vittuvacaṉa kōlākalaṉ eṉṉum ākkiyāḻvāṉukkum āḷavantārk kum kulōttuṅkaccōḻaṉ (?) avaikkaḷattil nikaḻnta tarukkattil, āḷavantār mēṟkūṟiya kaṟpiḻukkak kūṟṟaic cāṉṟu kāṭṭi, cōḻaṉ tēvi kaṟpiḻantavaḷ eṉṟu nāṭṭiyapōtu, aracaṉum araciyum uṭpaṭa avaiyōr aṉaivarum pārāṭṭiyatu, tamiḻaṉukku aḻiyāp paḻiyait taruvatām.
(5) vīṇ caṭaṅkukaḷ 
tī valañceytal, ammi mitittal, aruntati kāṭṭal mutaliya pala caṭaṅkukaḷ vīṇāṉavaiyākum.
ammi mitittal, akalikai cāvakkataiyai niṉaippittu maṇamakaḷai eccarikkum caṭaṅkākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. ammiyait tirumakaḷ taṅkum poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟākak koḷvatiṉāl, atai mitikkum vaḻakkam iyalpākat tamiḻarkkillai. atu kaṭṭukkaḻuttiyiṉ aṭaiyāḷameṉṟu tamiḻap peṇṭir kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ammiyum kuḻaviyum tāyaiyuñ cēyaiyuṅ kuṟittu maṇamakaḷ makappēṟṟai muṉṉōkkuvaṉa veṉṟum, cilar kūṟuva tuṇṭu. eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, camaiyaṟkiṉṟiyamaiyāta ammi u ṇavut taṭṭaṟṟa nalvāḻvaik kuṟippateṉṟu koḷvatu kuṟṟamākātu. ētōvoru naṟkuṟippiṉatāka maṇavaṟaiyiṟ koṇṭuvantu vaikkappeṟum ammikkum, aḻakiya kōlamiṭappeṟum āyiraṅkaṇṇup pāṉaikkum, poruttamāka ōr āriyattoṭarpukatai kaṭṭappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. akalikai cāvattaik kuṟippataṟkup potuvakaiyāṉa kal pōtumē! avaḷ kallākac cāvikkap paṭṭāḷ eṉṟallātu, ammiyākac cāvikkappaṭṭāḷ eṉṟu katai kūṟavillaiyē! mēlum, koṅkuvēḷāḷar tirumaṇattil maṇamaṉaikku veḷiyē ammi niṟuttappaṭṭu, nāṭṭukkal eṉap peyarpeṟṟup pūcaiyuṭaṉ vaṇaṅkappeṟukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, pirāmaṇar tirumaṇattil, maṇamakaḷ oru kallai mitikkumpōtu, maṇamakaṉ ō peṇṇē! ik kallai miti. itaippōl uṟutiyāyiru. uṉakkut tīṅku ceyya nāṭuvārai aḻittuviṭu. uṉ pakaivarai vel eṉṟu maṇamakaḷai nōkkik kūṟuvatum, iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
aruntati kāṭṭal eṉpatum āriyat toṭarpu karutiyatē. tamiḻa marapuppaṭi aruntati talaiyāya kaṟparaci yallaḷ. avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉākiya vaciṭṭamuṉivaṉ oḻukkattaippaṟṟi aiyuṟṟu, avaṉāl viṇmīṉākac cāvikkappaṭṭāḷ eṉṟu katai kūṟukiṉṟatu. tamiḻap pattiṉip peṇṭirō, kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu coṟṟiṟam pāmai, eṉṉuṅ koḷkaiyiṉar. ātalāl, ātimantiyār, pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyār, kaṇṇakiyār, āriyappaṭai kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ tēviyār, tilakavatiyār mutaliya ettuṇaiyō talaiyāya tamiḻakak kaṟput teyvaṅkaḷirukkavum, avarai viṭṭuviṭṭu aruntatiyai niṉaippittal, kaṉiyiruppak kāykavarn taṟṟē.
6) karaṇam viḷaṅkāmai 
makkaḷ āṟaṟivuṭaiyār. ataṉāl etaic ceyyiṉum aṟivōṭu ceytaṟkuriyar. oruvar vāḻkkaiyil talaiciṟanta nikaḻcciyāyum, iruvar iṉpa vāḻviṟku aṭikōluvatāyum, palar makiḻcciyuṟutaṟkuriya kāṭciyāyum, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ vantu kūṭuvatāyum, uḷḷa tirumaṇaccaṭaṅkaip purōkitaṉukkaṉṟip poruḷoṭu ōtu kiṉṟāṉā poruḷillātu uḷaṟu
kiṉṟāṉā eṉpataiyum aṟiya viyalāta nilaiyil, iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, kuruṭṭuttaṉamāka naṭattivaruvatu, nākarikattiṟkum paṇpāṭṭiṟkum muṟṟum muraṇāṉatām.
2. kulakkaṭṭuppāṭṭu mikai 
toḻilpaṟṟiya kulappākupāṭu toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻakattil iruntuvantatēṉum, tamiḻa iṉam oṟṟumai kulaintu ciṉṉapiṉṉamāyc citaitaṟkum, ayalār eḷitāyp paṭaiyeṭuttuvantu kaippaṟṟutaṟkum, vaḻivakuttatu, piṟkālattil piṟappoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttappaṭṭa varuṇācirama tarumam eṉṉum āriyamuṟaik kulappiriviṉaiyāl viḷainta kulaveṟiyē. itaṉālēyē, kuraṅkāṉālum kulattilē koḷ. paḻaṅkālait tūrkkātē, putukkālai veṭṭātē eṉap pala tīya koḷkaikaḷ piṟantaṉa. tirai kaṭalōṭiyun tirumikat tēṭu eṉṉum tāḷāṇmai mikka nāṭṭil, kaṭal tāṇṭakkūṭāteṉṟum, āṟu tāṇṭakkūṭāteṉṟum, kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
orē kulattiṟkuḷ tirumpattirumpa maṇañ ceytu vantatiṉāl aṟivāṟṟal mikka piḷḷaikaḷ arukip piṟantaṉa.
aṇṭāmai, tīṇṭāmai, kāṇāmai mutaliya kūṭṭaravuk koṭumai kaḷellām, iv iṭaikkālattiltāṉ talaivirittut tāṇṭavamāṭiṉa.
3. poruntā maṇamum vīṇcaṭaṅkum 
kulaveṟi mella mellac cuṟṟaveṟiyai uṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu. oru muṟaikārap peṇṇukku allatu āṇukku, eṅkē vēṟiṭattil maṇam muṭintuviṭukiṉṟatō eṉṉum accattiṉāl, piḷḷaip paruvattilēyē avarkku maṇam ceytu vaippatu vaḻakkamāyviṭṭatu. cilar toṭṭiṟ kuḻantaikkum maṇañ ceytu vaittatuṇṭu. atu toṭṭil maṇam eṉṉappaṭṭatu. orutti kaṉṉiyāy iṟantāl tīkkatiyaṭaivāḷ eṉṉum āriyakkoḷkaiyum, iḷamai maṇattiṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟṟu.
āṟiluñ cāvu nūṟiluñ cāvu ātalāl, maṇañceytu vaikkappaṭṭa piḷḷai maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷ pūppaṭaiyumuṉ iṟantuviṭuvatumuṇṭu. avaṉāl maṇakkappaṭṭa ciṟumi uṇmaiyil avaṉai maṇantirāviṭṭālum, maṇantatupōṉṟē karutappaṭṭuk kaimpeṇṇāyviṭuvāḷ. kaimpeṇ maṇañceyyuṅ kulamāyiṉ, avaṭku maṇavāḻkkaiyuṇṭu. atilum putup
peṇmaiyai iḻantēyiruppāḷ. kaimpeṇ maṇañceyyāk kulamāyiṉ, avaṭku maṇavāḻkkai illavēyillai. kālamellām amaṅkaliyāka, pūppaṭaintapiṉ kāmavuṇarcci yaṭakkum karaiyaṟṟa tuṉpattiṟkō karutappaṭṭu, oḻukkakkēṭṭiṟkō uriyavaḷāvāḷ. piṟa peṇṭirpōl oru kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭi vāḻavum, pūccūṭavum, maṅkala viṉaikaḷiṟ kalantukoḷḷavum, veḷippaṭaiyāyp piḷḷai peṟavum, iṉpanukaravum, urimaiyaṟṟavaḷāy, taṉ vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum ulaka vāḻkkaiyai oru ciṟaivāḻkkaiyum aḷaṟṟu (naraka) vāḻkkaiyumākavē kaḻittu, colloṇāta tuṉpuṟuvāḷ.
piḷḷai maṇamakaṉ taṉ ciṟu maṉaivi pūppaṭaintu taṉṉoṭu kūṭivāḻumvarai iṟavātiruppiṉum, nīṭittuvāḻum nilaimaiyillākkāl, mēṟkūṟiya tuṉpaṅkaḷē nērum. pūppaṭaintapiṉṉarē makaḷirkku maṇañ ceytuvaippiṉ, ittakaiya tuṉpaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu perumpālum tappiyuyvar.
piḷḷaip paruvattil maṇañceytu vaippatāl, maṉaivi pūppaṭainta vuṭaṉ kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭivāḻa nērum. uṭal muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭaiyumuṉ karukkoḷḷum makaḷir, talaimakap pēṟṟil iṟantuviṭuvatumuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam iṟavātiruppiṉum, piṟakkum piḷḷai uraṉuḷḷatāyum nīṭu vāḻiyāyum iruppatillai; tāyuṭalum taḷarntuviṭuvatuṇṭu;
pūppaṭaiyumuṉ maṇañceytu vaittālum pūppaṭaintavuṭaṉ maṇañceytu vaittālum, makaḷiriṉ iḷamaiyaip poṟutta varaiyil, iraṇṭum oṉṟē. pūppaṭaintavuṭaṉ ellā makaḷirkkum kāmavuṇarcci ēṟpaṭṭuviṭuvatillai. itaṉāl, maṇam nikaḻnta piṉpum kūṭṭanāḷ taḷḷivaikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu cērvu maṇam (cānti kaliyāṇam) allatu cērvu muḻuttam (cānti mukūrttam) eṉṉum vīṇ caṭaṅkiṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟṟu.
kāmak kuṟippum cērkkait tuṇivum illāta iḷamparuva āṇiṟkum peṇṇiṟkum maṇañceytu vaippatiṉālēyē, avaṟṟai avarkku ūṭṭumporuṭṭu, nalaṅkiṭal, uṇavūṭṭal, veṟṟilai maṭittuk koṭuttal, appaḷantaṭṭal, oṟṟaiyā iraṭṭaiyā piṭittal, pūcceṇṭeṟital, eḻuttāṇiyeṭuttal, oḷintu viḷaiyāṭal, mōtiram eṭuttal, piḷḷaiyār allatu pommai koṭuttal, ūñcalāṭal, kōpittukkoṇṭu pōṉa maṇamakaṉai aḻaittuvaral, mañcaḷ nīrāṭal mutaliya pala vīṇāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. maṇavāḻkkaikkēṟṟa vaḷarcciyaṭainta paruvattil makkaṭku maṇañceytu vaittāl, avar piṟar tuṇaiyiṉṟit tāmākavē kūṭi vāḻvar.
cila tamiḻakkulattār maṇavaṟaiyil tiraikaṭṭit tāli kaṭṭuvatu, makamatiyar in nāṭṭiṟku vantapiṉ ēṟpaṭṭa payaṉaṟṟa ceyalākum.
iṉi, vaḷarcciyaṭainta makkaṭku maṇañceytu vaittaviṭattum, avar viruppattaik kēḷātu peṟṟōr tam viruppappaṭiyē icaiyāta iḷaiñaṉaiyum iḷaiñaiyaiyum iṇaittu vaittu, piṉpu avar tuṉpuṟumpōtu tāramuṅ kuruvun talaiviti eṉṟum, kaḻuttil viḻunta mālai kaḻaṟṟa muṭiyātu eṉṟum, aṭuppil vaitta koḷḷi eṟintutāṉ tīravēṇṭum eṉṟum, kūṟuvatu avar maṭamaiyaik kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatē yākum.
iṉi maṇamakkaḷiṉ otta vaḷarcciyai oruciṟitum kavaṉiyātu, oru ciṟuvaṉukku oru perum peṇṇai maṇañceytu vaittu māmaṉē avaḷukkuk kaṇavaṉāvatum, oru muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭainta muraṭaṉaik kaṇṭu añcic cākumāṟu, avaṉukku oru maṅkaip paruvattu melliyaḷai maṇam puṇarppatum, iṭaikkālattu nikaḻntuvanta koṭuñ ceyalkaḷām.
paḻañcēra nāṭākiya malaiyāḷa nāṭṭil, pūppiṉmuṉ tālikaṭṭu eṉṉum poy maṇamum, pūppiṉpiṉ campantam eṉṉum meymmaṇamum, āka irumaṇam ovvoru peṇṇiṟkum cila kulattil nērntataṟkuk kāraṇam āriyamē.
III. tirumaṇac cīrtiruttam
1. cīrtirutta iyakkam
iṭaikkālattil tamiḻ marapiṟkovvāta pala āriya muṟaikaḷum caṭaṅkukaḷum tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷiṟ kalantuviṭṭataṉālum, avaṟṟāl tamiḻukkum pala tīmaikaḷ viḷaintatiṉālum, avaṟṟaik kaḷaintu tamiḻ maṇattait tiruttaṟkup pala cīrtiruttat talaivar tōṉṟiṉar. avaruḷ nāyakamāṉavar nālvar. avarāvār :
(1) cittūr mārkkacakāya āccāriyār
ivar ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil cittūr māvaṭṭattil tōṉṟiyavar; poṟkollar vakuppiṉar; vaṭamoḻi vēta itikāca purāṇaṅkaḷai naṉṟāyk kaṟṟavar. ivar, kammāḷar vicuvappirāmaṇar eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vētappaṭi vicuvappirāmaṇarē tirumaṇam naṭattivaikkavēṇṭumeṉṟum, pirāmaṇar makkaḷai ēmāṟṟit toṉṟutoṭṭu naṭantuvanta muṟaiyiṉai iṭaiyil māṟṟiviṭṭaṉareṉṟum, pōrāṭi, oru tirumaṇattai vaṭamoḻiyil naṭatti vaikkat toṭaṅkiyapōtu, pañcāṅkam kuṇṭaiyar eṉṉum pirāmaṇap purōkitar vantu taṭuttār. mārkkacakāya āccāriyār avarukku iṇaṅkāta tiṉāl kalakam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ataṉ viḷaivāy oru naṭuvar kuḻuk kūṭiṟṟu. ataṉ muṉṉilaiyil, pañcāṅkam kuṇṭaiyar viṉaviya viṉākkaṭkellām mārkkacakāya āccāriyār uṭaṉuṭaṉ takka viṭai viṭuttuviṭṭataṉāl, āccāriyār pakkam muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. ataṉpiṉṉum pañcāṅkam kuṇṭaiyar viṭāmal, muṟaiyē, kīḻ muṟaiyāḷar (Sub Magistrate) maṉṟattilum, māvaṭṭa maṉṟattilum, vaḻakkut toṭuttār. aṅkum viṉaviya vaṟṟiṟkellām takkavāṟu viṭuttuviṭṭataṉāl, āccāriyār pakkamē tīrppāyiṟṟu. ip pōrāṭṭamum veṟṟiyum, cittūr atālattuk kōrṭṭut tīrppu eṉṉum talaippil puttaka vaṭivāy veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷa.
(2) maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ 
ivar taṉittamiḻ iyakkat talaivarātalāl, āriya moḻiyai aṟavē akaṟṟi, tirumaṇak karaṇamoḻi muḻuvataiyum tūya tamiḻil amaittuk koṇṭār. vēḷvi vaḷarttalum tīvalam varutalum maṇavaṟaiyil maṅkalap poruḷ vaittalum, ivarāl vilakkappaṭavillai.
(3) tiru.vi.kaliyāṇacuntaraṉār 
ivarum maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟārē. āyiṉ, aṭikaḷ karaṇa moḻiyil maruntiṟkum vaṭacol kāṇpataritu; ivar moḻiyilō orōvōr iṭattil vaṭacoṟ kalakkum. ituvē ivartammuḷ vēṟṟumai.
eṉiṉum, piṟarmoḻipōl maṇippavaḷa mālaiyum paṉmaṇik kōvaiyumā yirātu, vaṭacol arukivarap peṟuvatē maṇavaḻakaṉār (kaliyāṇacuntaraṉār) moḻiyum eṉka.
(4) ī.vē. irāmacāmip periyār 
ivar, āriyak karaṇattaiyum maṇavaṟai yamaippaiyum kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭaiyum maṇaccaṭaṅkukaḷaiyum vilakki, potumakkaḷ tamiḻil vāḻkkai oppantattai vakuttut tantavar. ivar ceyta māṟutalkaḷuḷ, kaṭavuḷvaḻipāṭṭu vilakku nampā matattārālēyē yaṉṟi nampumatattārāl cīrtiruttamākak koḷḷappeṟātu.
iṅṅaṉam, mārkkacakāyar pirāmaṇiyattaiyum, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷum kaliyāṇacuntaraṉārum aḵtuṭaṉ āriya moḻiyaiyum, ī.vē.rā. avaṟṟōṭu vīṇ caṭaṅkukaḷaiyum, vilakkit tirumaṇak karaṇattait tiruttiyavarāvār.
2. peṟṟōr kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyavai 
maṇamakkaḷ perumpālum ulakiyalaṟiyā iḷamaiyarātalāṉum, maṇavāḻkkai āyiraṅkālattup payirātalāṉum iḷamai eḷitāy uṇarcci vayappaṭṭuk keṭṭuviṭutalāṉum, tirumaṇap poṟuppai muṟṟum maṇamakkaḷiṭattil viṭṭuviṭātu, peṟṟōr muṟpaṭavē viḻippākavum eccarikkaiyākavumiruntu piṉvarum paṉṉiru kaṭamaikaḷai āṟṟalvēṇṭum.
(1) pālnilai kavaṉittal 
cila peṟṟōr, āṇuṭampu koṇṭārellām āṇmaiyareṉṟum, peṇṇuṭampu koṇṭārellām peṇmaiyareṉṟum karuti, avar pāliyalpu aṟṟiruppataiyum tirintiruppataiyum kavaṉippatē yillai.
āṇmai tirintu peṇmai koṇṭavaṉ pēṭi; peṇmai tirintu āṇmai koṇṭavaḷ pēṭaṉ; iv viruvarkkum potup peyar pēṭu; irupālum allātatu ali. im mūvarkkum maṇamē vēṇṭiya tillai.
iṉi, irupāluḷḷum, pāluṇarcciyō cērkkai viruppamō oru ciṟitum illātavarumuṇṭu. avarkkum maṇam vēṇṭuvatillai.
āṇmai tiriyātavaruḷḷum pala tīya paḻakkaṅkaḷāl cērkkaiyāṟṟal iḻantavar cilar. avarkku maṇañceytuvaippiṉ, avar maṉaiviyar oḻukkaṅkeṭa vēṇṭum; allatu kaṇavarkkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭuk kālamellām erintu vēkavēṇṭum.
orōvoru peṇ orupōtum pūppaṭaivatillai. avarkkum maṇam vēṇṭuvatillai.
makkaḷtokai mikkuḷḷa ikkālattil, maṇaṅkaḷai ettuṇaik kuṟaittal muṭiyumō attuṇaik kuṟaittal vēṇṭum.
(2) paṉṉiru poruttam pārttal 
i. kātaṟ poruttam 
maṇamakkaṭku mutaṉmaiyāka vēṇṭum poruttam ituvē. iḵtillāviṭiṉ, ēṉaic ciṟappellāmiruntum ētum payaṉillai. ātalāl, maṇamakkaḷ icaivu peṟṟē avar peṟṟōr avarkku maṇañceytu vaittal vēṇṭum.
ii. uṭalnalap poruttam 
maṇamakkaḷ iṉpan tuyttaṟkum illaṟam naṭattaṟkum iṉṟiyamai yātatu uṭalnalam. cila nōykaḷ maṟaivāyirukkum allatu maṟaikkap paṭṭirukkum. ātalāl, takka maruttuvap paṇṭi taraik koṇṭu maṇamakkaḷai nōṭṭañceytal vēṇṭum. allākkāl, oruvarāl iṉṉoruvar nōyppaṭuvatuṭaṉ, avarkkup piṟakkum piḷḷaikaḷum vaḻivaḻi nōyppaṭṭu varuntavēṇṭiyirukkum.
iii. oḻukkap poruttam 
nalloḻukkattil tuppuravum (cuttamum) aṭaṅkum. oḻukkap poruttamillākkāl, auvaiyārk kamutu paṭaikkac coṉṉavaṉum kuṇṭalakēciyumpōl tuṉpuṟa nērum.
iv. karuttup poruttam 
kaṭci matam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ karuttottalum, vēṟupaṭiṉ av vēṟu pāṭṭiṟku iṭantaralum karuttupporuttamām. uyarnta paṇpāṭṭiṉarāyiṉ, kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum vevvēṟu matattiṉarāyu mirukkalām.
v. uṇṭip poruttam 
maravuṇavum pulāluṇavum ākiya iraṇṭaṉuḷḷum oṉṟil ottirutta lum, ovvātu vēṟupaṭiṉ av vēṟupāṭṭiṟku iṭantaralum uṇṭip poruttamām.
vi. akavaip poruttam 
maṇamakkaḷ uṭal nalvaḷarcciyaṭaintapiṉ maṇantāltāṉ, naṉmakap pēṟuṇṭāvatuṭaṉ tāykkuñ cētamiṉṟām. atē camaiyattil, iḷamai kaḻiyumuṉ maṇantāltāṉ, iṉpacciṟappum piṟa vacatikaḷum uṇṭākum. ātalāl, piṉvarumāṟu maṇamakkaḷ akavai kavaṉikkappeṟiṉ nalamām.
	ciṟṟellai	iṭaiyellai	pērellai
maṇamakaṉ akavai	20	22	25
maṇamakaḷ akavai	16	18	20
in nāṭu veppa nāṭātalāl, kuḷirnāṭukaḷiṟpōl iḷamaiyar nīṇṭanāḷ maṇavātiruttal iyalātu. ātalāl, makkaḷ pērellaiyakavai aṭaintavuṭaṉ, peṟṟōr avarkku maṇañceytu vaittal nallatu.
takka paruvattil maṇañceytu vaiyāmaiyāl, iḷaiñarum iḷaiñaiyarum pala tīya paḻakkaṅkaṭku āḷāyviṭukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl iḷaiñar koṭiya nōykaḷai aṭaivatuṭaṉ, cērkkai yāṟṟalaiyum iḻantuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṇmaiyoṉṟē āṭavarkkuc ciṟappu. ataṉāl iṉpuṟun tiṟaṉmaṭṭumaṉṟi akakkaraṇa valimaiyum vāḻnāḷum mikukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, āṇmaiyiḻantuviṭiṉ āṭavaṉukku vāḻkkaiyillai. ataṉ
piṉ, irunitikkiḻavaṉāyiṉum īṭilā arampaiyaip peṟiṉum eṉṉa payaṉ?
mēlum, pinti maṇañceyvatāl, piḷḷaikaḷun talaiyeṭukkāmal peṟṟōriṉ mutumaiyiṟ ciṉṉatuñciṟiyatumā yiruntu, kuṭumpam mikamika iṭarppaṭukiṉṟatu.
ivaṟṟaiyellām nōkki, iṉimēlāyiṉum, peṟṟōr tam piḷḷaikaṭkut takka paruvattil maṇañ ceytuvaippārāka.
vii. uruvap poruttam 
maṇamakkaḷ vaḷarttiyilum parumaṉilum aḻakutarattilum ottiruppatu uruvap poruttamām. maṇamakaṉilum maṇamakaḷ kuṭṭaiyāyum olliyāyum irukkalām. itaṟku māṟāyiruttal kūṭātu.
aḻaku iṉpattai mikuttuk kāṭṭuvatāl, aḻakuṇarcciyuḷḷa maṇamakkaṭku iyaṉṟavarai aḻakuḷḷa vāḻkkaittuṇaiyē amarttal vēṇṭum.
maṇamakkaḷ uṟuppuk kuṟaiyillātiruttalum uruvap poruttamām. cila peṟṟōr, tam makaṉiṉ allatu makaḷiṉ uṟuppuk kuṟaiyai āṭaiyaṇi
kaḷāl maṟaittu vaippatuṇṭu. atai naṉṟāyk kavaṉittal vēṇṭum.
viii. kalvip poruttam 
maṇamakkaḷ iruvarum kaṟṟōrāyirukkalām; allatu maṇamakaṉ maṭṭum kaṟṟirukkalām. maṇamakaḷ kaṟṟum maṇamakaṉ kallātum iruttal kūṭātu. maṇamakaḷ maṇamakaṉiṉum mikutiyākak kaṟṟiruttalum, cerukkiṟkum ottuḻaiyāmaikkum kāraṇamāyiṉ naṉṟaṉṟu.
maṇamakaḷ iṉṟiyamaiyātu kaṟṟirukkak vēṇṭiya nūlkaḷ (Sciences) uṭalnūlum, (Physiology) karunūlum, (Embryology) piḷḷaivaḷarppu nūlum ākum; kalaikaḷ (Arts) camaiyalum taiyalum icaiyumākum. poruttam pārppatu maṇamakkaḷ icaintu iṉitāy illaṟam naṭakkaṟporuṭṭēyātaliṉ, av icaivupāṭṭiṟkuk kāraṇamāka ellā nilaimaiyum poruttamē.
oru maṉaikkiḻattiyār ettuṇaiyuyarnta kalvip paṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟṟiruppiṉum, camaiyal teriyātiruppiṉ, avar kalvi niṟaivuṟṟatākak koḷḷappaṭātu. kaṭṭaḻakellām iḷamaik kālattiltāṉ iṉpuṟuttik kuṟai maṟaikkum. iṭaimaiyilum mutumaiyilum iṉpuṟuttuvatu aṟucuvai yuṇṭiyē. tāykkuppiṉ tāram eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. aṟucuvai yuṇṭi amarntillā ḷūṭṭa eṉpatu nālaṭiyār (1). vaḷḷuvar taputāra nilaiyiṟ pāṭiya kaiyaṟam,
aṭiciṟ kiṉiyāḷē aṉpuṭaiyāḷē
eṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu.
maṉaikkiḻattiyār araca allatu peruñ celvak kuṭiyaic cērntavarāyiruntu, camaiyaṟku oru maṭainūl vallāṉai  amarttik koṇṭālum, aṉṟum avarkku ak kalai terintiruppatu naṉṟēyām. mālaikkālattilum kaḷaippunērattilum maṉaiviyiṉ miṭaṟṟicai allatu karuvi icai kaṇavaṟku iṉpūṭṭum.
iṉi, maṇamakkaḷ iruvarum tāymoḻiyil ottiruppatum kalvip poruttattiṉ pāṟpaṭum. moḻiyē kalvi vāyilātaliṉ, iruvar moḻiyum vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉ, oruvar moḻiyai oruvar viraintu kaṟṟukkoḷḷal vēṇṭum.
ix. muṟaip poruttam
maṇamakkaḷ uṟaviṉarāyiṉ, maṇamuṟaiyiṉarā yiruttal vēṇṭum. muṟaiyal maṇattiṉāl (Incest) naṉmakappēṟu uṇṭāvatillai. kuṭikkum iḻukkām.
iṉi, akkaiyār makaḷai maṇattal muṟaimaṇamāyiṉum, neruṅkiya vuṟavāyiruttalāl atuvum vilakkaṟpālatē. kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyariṉ aratta uṟavu neruṅka neruṅka uṭal noymaiyum mati maḻukkamum, akala akala uṭaluramum mati viḷakkamum, avartam makkaṭku amaiyum eṉpatai aṟitalvēṇṭum.
x. viṉaittiṟap poruttam
maṇamakkaḷ iruvarum cūḻcci valimaiyilum cuṟucuṟuppilum ottiruppatu, viṉaittiṟap poruttamām. avaruḷ oruvar viraimatiyum tāḷāṇmaiyuṭaiyavarāyirukka, iṉṉoruvar manta matiyum cōmpalu
muṭaiyavarāyiruppiṉ avartam illaṟa vāḻkkai oṟṟumaiyum iṉpamum uṭaiyatāyirātu.
xi. toḻiṟ poruttam
maṇamakkaḷ iruvarum orē toḻilai allatu toṭarpuḷḷa toḻil kaḷaic ceytal, toḻiṟ poruttamām. toḻil vēṟupaṭiṉum māṟupaṭal kūṭātu.
xii. kuṭumpanilaip poruttam 
ūril oruvaṉē tōḻaṉ, ārumaṟṟatē tāram eṉpatālum, maṇamakaḷ celva mikkavaḷāyiṉ maṇamakaṉukku naṉṟēyātalālum, celvap poruttam ciṟappāyk kavaṉittaṟkuriya toṉṟaṉṟām. āyiṉum, iḷavaracaṉum kūlikkāriyumpōl, allatu kūlikkāraṉum iḷavaraciyumpōl, maṇamakkaḷ celvanilai aḷaviṟantu vēṟupaṭiṉ, avar oruvarukkoruvar vāḻkkaittuṇaiyāy iṇaittu vaikkappaṭaṟkuriyavarākār. kuṭumpa nilaiyaip piṟappu eṉṟu kūṟiṉum okkum.
iṅṅaṉam paṉṉiru poruttamum pārātu ceytu vaikkappaṭum allatu ceyyappaṭum maṇaṅkaḷ,
āyntāyntu koḷḷātāṉ kēṇmai kaṭaimuṟai
tāṉcān tuyaran tarum	 (792)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaṭ kilakkāṉa naṭpuppōl, tuṉpan taruvaṉavēyām.
piṟappē kuṭimai āṇmai yāṇṭō(ṭu)
uruvu niṟutta kāma vāyil
niṟaiyē aruḷē uṇarvoṭu tirueṉa
muṟaiyuṟak kiḷanta oppiṉatu vakaiyē
eṉpatu, tolkāppiyam (meyppāṭṭiyal, 25) kūṟum patiṉ poruttamākum.
itaṉ poruḷ :otta  piṟappum, otta oḻukkamum, otta āṇmaiyum, otta paruvamum, otta uruvanilaiyum, otta aṉpum, otta niṟaiyum, otta aruḷum, otta aṟivum, otta celvamum eṉat talaimakaṉum talaimakaḷum (kātalaṉum kātaliyum) ottirukkum pakutikaḷ pattu vakaiya eṉṟavāṟu.
ivaṟṟuḷ, niṟaiyum aruḷum oḻukkattuḷ aṭaṅkum.
otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum kāṇpa
mikkōṉ āyiṉum kaṭivarai yiṉṟē
eṉṟu tolkāppiyak kaḷaviyal (2) kūṟuvatāl, perumpālum maṇamakkaḷ palavakaiyilum ottirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatum, av oppumaiyē nīṭitta iṉpavāḻkkaikku ētuvāṉateṉpatum, peṟappaṭum.
(3) kulaveṟi koḷḷāmai 
tamiḻaraip pirittuk keṭuttaṟkeṉṟē, piṟappoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa kulappiriviṉai ayalārāṟ pukuttappaṭṭateṉṟum, atu nūlutti paṭṭaṟivukaṭku muṟṟum muraṇāṉateṉṟum ulaka muḻumaiyiṉum innāvalan tēyattilēyē atu uḷḷateṉṟum, tiruvaḷḷuvar tirumūlar mutaliya periyōrellārā lum kaṇṭikkappaṭṭateṉṟum, uṇarāta tamiḻar, iṉṉum kulavuṇarcci yilēyē tiḷaittu varukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl, ovvoru kulattārum tamakkuḷḷēyē maṇantu, tamiḻiṉattil pēraṟivuc cuṭarkaḷ peruvāriyāyp piṟavātapaṭi taṭuttuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. atōṭu tamiḻarkkuḷ oṟṟumaiyum illātu pōkiṉṟatu.
oṉṟupaṭṭāluṇṭu vāḻvu - iṅku
oṟṟumai yiṉṟēl aṉaivarkkup tāḻvu
itaittamiḻ mākāṇa muṉṉēṟṟam paṟṟiyāvatu kavaṉittal vēṇṭum.
nīṇṭakālamākat toṭarntuvarum kulamuṟaiyait tiṭumeṉṟu nīkkuvatu iyalātēṉum, uyarttappaṭṭōrkkum tāḻttappaṭṭōrkkum itupōtu tuppuravu paṟṟi ēṟṟattāḻvu uḷḷatēṉum, vēḷāḷarum mutaliyārum pōlvārum, maṟavarum iṭaiyarum pōlvārum, iraṭṭiyārum nāyuṭuvum pōlvarum, tammuḷ maṇantukoḷḷalāmē!
tamiḻariṉ palvēṟu peruṅkulaṅkaḷaṉṟi, (nāṭum ūrumākiya) iṭam, uṇporuḷ, uṭai, ciṟu teyvam, koḷkai, caṭaṅku, ceyporuḷ, ciṟappu nikaḻcci, kuṇam, niṟam, kuṭuminilai, vaḻakkam, aṇi, tālivakai, moḻi, aluval, paṭṭam, kulavaricai, ciṉṉam, makkaḷ tokai, tokuppu, kalappu, tōṟṟakkatai, tiṟamai, muṟaippeyar mutaliya paṟpala vakaippaṟṟip pirintu
kiṭakkum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa poruntāk kulappirivukaḷiṟ palavum, akamaṇa vakuppukkaḷākavē yiruntuvaruvatu, tamiḻaṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku māperuntaṭai
yātaliṉ, kalvikaṟṟa tamiḻa iḷaiñar kuruṭṭuttaṉamum kuṟukiya nōkkumuḷḷa kulamutiyar coṟkoḷḷātu, atai viraintu takarntateṟiyakkaṭavar.
(4) paricam vāṅkāmai 
peṇṇiṟkup paricam kēṭpatu ōraḷavu vilai kūṟuvatu pōṉṟirutta lāl, celvap peṟṟōr ataṉaik kēḷātiruttal ciṟappām. ēḻaip peṟṟōrāyiṉ, maṇaccelavu nōkki oru tokai kēṭpatu kuṟṟamākātu.
iṉi, peṇṇiṟkuk kēṭpataṟkup patilāka maṇavāḷap piḷḷaikkuk kēṭpatu, iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉatāyum, ekkāraṇattālum carimaippaṭutta muṭiyātatāyum irukkiṉṟatu. ivvulakattil oruvaṉ peṟakkūṭiya pēriṉpap pēṟu peṇṇē. akattaḻakum puṟattaḻakum oruṅkēyamainta arumaip peṇṇirukkavum avaḷai viṭṭuviṭṭu, kāciṟkācaippaṭṭu aḻakiliyai maṇappatu, illaṟa iṉpattaiyum vāḻkkai vacatiyaiyum paṇattiṟku viṟpatu pōṉṟatē.
(5) nāḷum vēḷaiyum pārāmai 
nāḷ ceyvatu nallār ceyyār, eṉpatu nam muṉṉōriṉ koḷkaiyēyāṉiṉum, aṟiviyal (viññāṉam) vaḷarcciyaṭaintuḷḷa iklattiṟku atu ēṟkātu.
iṭam eṉpatu eṅṅaṉam eṅkum parantu taṉṉaḷavil vēṟupāṭaṟṟatō, aṅṅaṉamē kālam eṉpatum eṉṟum parantu taṉṉaḷavil vēṟupāṭaṟṟatām. pakaliravum avaṟṟāl nāḷum vēḷaiyum ēṟpaṭuvataṟkuk kāraṇam, katiravaṉ tōṟṟamaṟaivu allatu ñālattiṉ (pūmiyiṉ) cuḻaṟciyē. iṟaivaṉ,
aṇṭap pakutiyiṉ uṇṭaip piṟakkam
aḷapparun taṉmai vaḷapperuṅ kāṭci
oṉṟaṉuk koṉṟu niṉṟeḻil pakariṉ
nūṟṟoru kōṭiyiṉ mēṟpaṭa virintaṉa
ilnuḻai katiriṉ nuṇaṇup puraiyac
ciṟiya vākap periyōṉ	(tiruvācakam, tiruvaṇṭappakuti, 1-6)
ātaliṉ, nāḷuṅkōḷum avaṉ āṇaikkaṭaṅkiyē naṭakkum. avaṉaṉṟi aṇuvum acaiyātu, `nāḷuṅkōḷumē nallatu ceyyum' eṉṟu nampuvār, kaṭavuḷai nampātavarum, avaratu
eṭṭut ticaiyum patiṉāṟu kōṇamum eṅkumoṉṟāy
muṭṭit tatumpi muḷaitteḻu cōti ttaṉmai
aṟiyātavarumē yāvar.
tiruñāṉa campantar maṅkaiyarkkaraciyāriṉ vēṇṭukōṭkiṇaṅkic camaṇarai yoḻikka maturai ceṉṟapōtu, iṉṟu nāḷuṅkōḷum naṉṟā yillāmaiyāl tāṅkaḷ maturai cella vēṇṭā eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracar taṭukka, avar, 
 vēyuṟu tōḷipaṅkaṉ viṭamuṇṭakaṇṭaṉ mikanalla vīṇaitaṭavi
 mācaṟu tiṅkaḷkaṅkai muṭimēlaṇinteṉ uḷamē pukuntavataṉāl
nāyiṟu tiṅkaḷcevvāy putaṉviyāḻam veḷḷicaṉi pāmpiraṇ 				ṭumuṭaṉē
ācaṟu nallanalla avainallanalla aṭiyāra varkkumikavē''
 
eṉpoṭu kompoṭāmai ivaimār pilaṅka erutēṟi yēḻai
	yuṭaṉē

	vataṉāl

	nāḷkaḷ avaitāṉ
aṉpoṭu nallanalla avainallanalla aṭiyā ravarkku
	mikavē
eṉṟu pāṭicceṉṟu muḻu veṟṟi kaṇṭār. innilaimai campantarpōlum periyārkkumaṭṭumaṉṟu, uṇmaiyaṉpuḷḷa ellārkkum urittām.
nāḷaṉṟu pōkip puḷḷiṭai taṭpap
pataṉaṉṟu pukkut tiṟaṉaṉṟu moḻiyiṉum
vaṟitu peyarkuva rallar neṟikoḷap
pāṭāṉ ṟiraṅku maruvip
pīṭukeḻu malaiyaṟ pāṭi yōrē      	(puṟam.124)
eṉṟu, celliṉuntappāta malaiyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāriyiṉ vaḷḷaṉmaiyaip pukaḻntuḷḷār kapilar.
nāḷkōḷkaḷiṉ iyakkattāl maḻaiyum maḻaiyiṉmaiyum pōla naṉmai tīmai viḷaivatu uṇmaiyāyiṉum, avviḷaivai av iyakkat tiṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa ellām valla iṟaivaṉ ēṟpāṭākak koḷvatallatu, ñālampōl uyiraṟṟa aḵṟiṇaiyiṭapporuḷkaḷāya nāḷkōḷkaḷiṉ ceyalākak koḷvatu eṅṅaṉam poruntum? tamiḻnāṭṭuk kīḻaikkaraip puyaṟcētattāl tuṉpuṟṟa makkaṭku ceṉṉai mākāṇa mutalamaiccar matitaku kāmarāca nāṭār pukaivaṇṭi vāyilāy naṭaippirakāram aṉuppiṉāreṉiṉ, am mutalamaiccarkku naṉṟi kūṟuvatallatu appukaivaṇṭikku naṉṟi kūṟuvatu poruntumō?
ovvoru naṉmuyaṟcikkum maṅkalaviṉaikkum, nāḷuṅ kiḻamaiyum ōraiyum vēḷaiyum pārttup pārttu eṉṟum accattōṭēyē vāḻvatāl, intiyar carācari vāḻnāḷ kuṟaintum; avaṟṟaippārāta mēṉāṭṭār vāḻnāḷ kūṭiyum uḷḷaṉa. ivvuṇmaiyaik kīḻvarum carācari vāḻnāṭ paṭṭiyiṟ kāṇka.
